### Decision 57 — Educational Increment 007L (Mathematics Teaching Architecture) closed: PASS WITH FINDINGS, architecture approved and frozen
**Decision:** Educational Increment 007L completed the bounded Mathematics Teaching Architecture proof — MODEL, Guided step reveal (progressive reveal of the question's own real `workingSteps`, single scored final submission), Independent Practice (unchanged), and misconception-mapped Wrong-Answer Remediation, reusing the existing subject-agnostic `recordOutcome`/`applyAttemptOutcome`/`supportTier` evidence and mastery pipeline unmodified — for four representative Mathematics families: `mr01-missing-operand`, `mr04-best-value`, `mr03-angle-ratio`, `mr01-measurement-conversion`. The production implementation (commit `258c853`) was independently verified this session: automated suite (250/250 then), TypeScript clean, Copy Quality Guard clean, production build clean, bank-wide Mathematics/Decision 55 regression clean (166/166 across 146 rows), production counts unchanged from the pre-007L baseline, and live production verification via the real learner Practice pathway across 5 fresh sessions (40 questions) — `mr04-best-value` and `mr03-angle-ratio` walked through end-to-end (MODEL, Guided reveal, Independent toggle, remediation on a deliberate wrong answer, correct scoring both modes); `mr01-missing-operand` and `mr01-measurement-conversion` confirmed via fresh-production automated script and unit tests only, not a direct live UI walkthrough this session (each has only 4 live rows out of ~146, so did not surface across the sampled sessions). The non-proof-family firewall held with no exception across 30+ non-proof-family live questions: zero MODEL/Guided UI rendered for any of them. **The Founder/Product leadership accepts 007L's final verdict as PASS WITH FINDINGS.** The underlying four-family Mathematics teaching architecture is **APPROVED and FROZEN** as the basis for future Mathematics teaching completion. This approval does **NOT** authorise immediate expansion to further Mathematics families, additional siblings, EASY/HARD variants, or any other content beyond the four proof families as they stand. Supported-versus-independent evidence protection (the `supportTier` gate in `lib/ali/mastery.ts`: a supported/guided correct answer can never by itself newly reach `"mastered"`) remains mandatory and unmodified. No eligibility, content, or Mock state changed during 007L or its verification — production counts (TOTAL 264, Practice Eligible 247, Mathematics Practice Eligible 141, English Practice Eligible 106, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0) are byte-identical to the pre-007L baseline, confirmed by direct fresh query. Wider Mathematics teaching implementation is explicitly deferred until the separate Angel Product Experience and Visual Audit has been completed.
**Rationale:** Closes the governance gap this session's own verification surfaced: commit `258c853` was pushed to `origin/main` and deployed to production (Vercel, Ready, dated 2026-08-14) before this decision was recorded, even though the design doc's own Part 10/11 states the proof "STOPS here for Founder review" and records no pre-selected review decision. This entry records that gap accurately rather than silently backfilling it as if a decision had existed all along — the deployment is real and independently verified as correct and safe, but the accompanying Decision-log entry that every other 007-series activation in this project carries (Decisions 50–56) did not exist until now. Recorded here so a future session does not need to re-derive or question this sequence.
**Date:** 2026-08-16
**Implications:** The Mathematics teaching architecture (MODEL / Guided step reveal / Independent / Remediation, family-keyed lookup in `mathsTeachingContent.ts`) is now the settled reference pattern for any future Mathematics teaching work, but no further family may be added to `MATHS_FAMILY_TEACHING_CONTENT` under this decision alone — each future family requires its own certification against the Part 4 evidence standard (007L design doc) and its own Founder/Product authorisation, not a blanket extension. No 007M, Batch 5, Wave 3, or new-subject work is authorised by this decision. The next authorised programme is the Angel Product Experience and Visual Audit, led separately by Product leadership; this decision does not pre-empt any of its design choices.

### Decision 58 — Applied Reasoning is current-excluded from the CSSE English paper (2025 Entry onward); current CSSE preparation is English + Mathematics only
**Decision:** With effect from September 2024 (2025 Entry onward), the CSSE English paper no longer contains Applied Reasoning questions — Founder-confirmed from current official CSSE information (CSSE Completion Programme Phase A, Part 1). Applied Reasoning (competency `AR-01`, Question Type `QT-AR-01`) is reclassified as **HISTORICAL CSSE EVIDENCE, NOT CURRENT CSSE EXAMINATION CONTENT**. Current CSSE preparation is **English (Comprehension + Continuous Writing) and Mathematics**. This resolves, in the Founder's favour, an evidence conflict this session's own audit had surfaced but explicitly declined to resolve unilaterally: `CSSE_EXAMINATION_BLUEPRINT.md` §5 had flagged the removal from a single, uncorroborated secondary source (now upgraded to HIGH confidence, Founder-confirmed), while `CSSE_FULL_MOCK_STRUCTURE_DECISION_V1.md` had classified Applied Reasoning as "STABLE ACROSS YEARS" from its own primary 2021-2023 evidence base and included it in a recommended target Mock structure (now corrected via an in-document notice, historical figures left unedited).
A repository-wide search for "Applied Reasoning"/"AR-01"/"QT-AR" found ~60 files. The large majority — `ASSESSMENT_BRAIN_V1.md` and its sibling `docs/intelligence/` framework documents, and the dated `knowledge/` programme-history documents — are faithful historical transcriptions of the real 2021-2023 exam papers (Assessment Brain V1's own Knowledge Asset base) or dated snapshots of past work packages; these make no current-status claim and are **left completely unedited**, per the explicit instruction that historical evidence must not be deleted or rewritten. Two documents made forward-looking, current/future-implementation-affecting claims and were corrected with an explicit, dated notice (not a silent edit, historical figures untouched): `CSSE_EXAMINATION_BLUEPRINT.md` §5 (confidence upgraded, Correction recorded) and `CSSE_FULL_MOCK_STRUCTURE_DECISION_V1.md` (notice added at the top; Applied Reasoning excluded from any current/future Mock structure).
Two **live, current, learner/parent-facing code defects** were found and fixed — confirmed by direct trace, not assumed from the prior report: `lib/learningEngine/assessmentBrainMap.ts`'s `ALL_COMPETENCY_IDS` and `ALL_ASSESSMENT_COMPONENTS` (the arrays every readiness/diagnostics/recommendation/revision-planning consumer iterates, not `COMPETENCIES` directly) included AR-01/"Applied Reasoning", which meant `components/learningEngine/ReadinessSummary.tsx` (rendered on the main Learning Report, Practice, Mock Exam, and both parent readiness routes) showed a permanent, structurally-unfixable "Applied Reasoning: Not Yet Evidenced" card, and `components/learningEngine/DiagnosticOverview.tsx` listed AR-01 as a permanent "coverage gap" chip — both for a component that no longer exists in the current exam and therefore could never accrue evidence. Fixed by excluding AR-01/"Applied Reasoning" from these two arrays only (`COMPETENCIES["AR-01"]` itself, `QUESTION_TYPE_PRIMARY_COMPETENCY["QT-AR-01"]`, and `lib/ali/labels.ts`'s AR-01 label are all left unedited — historical record, not deleted). 5 new regression tests added (`tests/lib/learningEngine/assessmentBrainMap.test.ts`), proving both live surfaces are clean and the historical transcription survives unedited. Also corrected: stale "AR-01 remains deferred (Gate 3)" wording in `app/learning-intelligence/founder-validation/csse/page.tsx` (a Founder-facing validation tool), which incorrectly implied future authoring was pending rather than the content being obsolete.
**Rationale:** An assessment component that no longer exists in the current exam must never be presented to a real parent or child as an active, permanently-unmet "gap" in their child's preparation — this is a genuine trust/accuracy risk on the product's primary readiness-communication surfaces, not merely a documentation nicety. Fixing it at the two root-cause arrays, rather than deleting the historical competency/Question-Type/label entries, preserves Assessment Brain V1's own evidentiary discipline (its historical tables remain a faithful 2021-2023 record) while stopping every live consumer from treating obsolete content as current.
**Date:** 2026-08-16
**Implications:** No Mathematics/English/Writing/Mock content or eligibility changed. A full, formal Assessment Brain V1.1 (with its own numbered Correction Log entry, per `ASSESSMENT_BRAIN_V1.md` §10) remains a separate, not-yet-authorised work package — this decision closes the live-product-behaviour risk without pre-empting that larger, self-governed documentation action. Any future Mock Weekend implementation (Phase F) must exclude Applied Reasoning from its structure. Any future Practice/content work must not allocate supply or teaching effort to AR-01/QT-AR-01.

### Decision 59 — Mock Content Firewall rule made structurally authoritative: `practice_eligible != mock_eligible`, enforced at every current Mock route, not merely intended
**Decision:** **AUTHORITATIVE FIREWALL RULE:** Mock content may only enter a genuine Mock assessment through an explicitly Mock-governed retrieval path (`lib/ali/questionBank.ts`'s `fetchMockEligibleQuestionBank()`, which filters strictly to `eligibility_status = "mock_eligible"` AND `active = true`). Practice eligibility must never imply Mock eligibility. `mock_eligible` remains 0 in production.
A full Mock architecture inventory was built by direct code trace this session (not accepted from the prior baseline report unverified, per explicit instruction):

| Route | Data source | Eligibility filter | Persistence | Reachability | Firewall status (before this decision) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `app/learning-intelligence/mock-exam/page.tsx` | `ali_question_bank` via `fetchMockEligibleQuestionBank` | `mock_eligible` + `active` | `recordReadinessSnapshot` + `saveMockResult`, real evidence pipeline | Linked from Mock Centre hub for CSSE-pathway learners | Already correct |
| `app/mocks/adaptive/{gl,english,maths,vocabulary}/page.tsx` (4 routes) | `ali_question_bank` via `fetchQuestionBank(subject, "gl")` — **the general Practice fetcher, not the firewalled one** | None — every non-provisional status, including `practice_eligible` | `startMockAttempt`/`completeMockAttempt` (Mock Attempt Ledger, real, persisted `MockResult`) | Reachable in production today: linked from `/reasoning`, which is linked from the primary bottom-nav "Practise" item for every non-CSSE pathway (GL/CEM/ISEB/Independent/no-pathway-selected) | **DEFECT, CONFIRMED** — see below |
| `app/mocks/[pathway]/page.tsx` | Static `data/{verbal-reasoning,non-verbal-reasoning,spatial-reasoning,numerical-reasoning,maths}.ts` — never touches `ali_question_bank` | N/A (not a firewall question — this route cannot leak Practice content because it never reads that table) | `saveMockResult` (`lib/mockProgress.ts`, localStorage) | Linked from Mock Centre hub for GL/CEM/ISEB legacy cards | Not a firewall defect (different problem: ungoverned static content, unrelated to `eligibility_status`) |
| `app/mock-test/page.tsx` | Static `data/lessons.ts` + `data/maths.ts` | N/A, same reason as above | Legacy `completeLesson()` | **Orphaned** — zero real inbound `href`/`Link` navigation found anywhere in `app/`/`components/` (the `"mock-test"` string elsewhere in the codebase is an unrelated progress-tracking category key, not a route link — checked and ruled out explicitly) | Not a firewall defect; dead route |
| `app/learning-intelligence/founder-validation/csse/page.tsx` | `ali_question_bank` via `fetchQuestionBank(subject, "csse-founder-validation")` | None, but structurally moot | Real evidence pipeline + Mock Attempt Ledger, explicitly disclosed on its own intro screen | Founder-only validation tool | **Not a genuine Mock route** (see rationale) — classified accurately per Part 2's own instruction, not forced into the Mock architecture |

**Confirmed defect:** the 4 `/mocks/adaptive/*` routes call `fetchQuestionBank()`, not `fetchMockEligibleQuestionBank()`, despite persisting real `MockResult`s (i.e. they are genuine Mock routes, not Practice). Not exploitable in production to date only because every `ali_question_bank` row is tagged `pathway: ["csse"]` or `["csse-founder-validation"]` — zero rows carry `pathway: "gl"`, the value these routes hardcode — so they have only ever served synthetic fallback fixtures. This was a live, reachable architectural gap: any future GL-pathway content authoring would have been silently servable as "Mock" the moment it existed, with no code change required on the routes' part.
**`founder-validation/csse` classification:** not a genuine Mock route. It reads a wholly separate pathway tag (`"csse-founder-validation"`, migration 021) that the real Mock (`mock-exam`, pathway `"csse"`) never reads and vice versa — structurally isolated by pathway, not by eligibility status. Its own header comment already discloses this is a controlled, non-production Founder-only slice, explicit on its own intro screen. Left unmodified — forcing it through `fetchMockEligibleQuestionBank` would break its actual, disclosed purpose (testing the evidence pipeline against a small fixed validation set) for no firewall benefit, since it cannot reach real Practice content regardless.
**Correction made:** all 4 `/mocks/adaptive/*` routes switched from `fetchQuestionBank` to `fetchMockEligibleQuestionBank`. Zero behaviour change in production today (all four already fall back to their existing, disclosed synthetic fixtures when the bank returns empty — confirmed identical before/after, since `mock_eligible` is 0 either way) — the fix is purely structural, closing the gap for any future content growth. `lib/ali/questionBank.ts` itself (the two functions and their contract) is unmodified; this is a call-site fix, not an architecture change, satisfying the "prefer one canonical Mock-eligible retrieval mechanism" instruction without building anything new.
**Regression protection:** `tests/lib/ali/mockContentFirewall.test.ts`, 9 tests, exercising the real exported `fetchQuestionBank`/`fetchMockEligibleQuestionBank` functions against an in-memory fake Postgrest client (no live DB dependency) — the 8 properties Part 2 specified (Practice-alone/Provisional/Provisional exclusion, Mock-eligible-only inclusion, empty-supply safety, no-general-fetcher source-level check across all 5 real Mock routes, growth-invariance, subject-isolation, non-delegation) plus one additional regression (retired/`active=false` rows excluded even if `mock_eligible`).
**Rationale:** The directive's own authoritative rule — practice_eligible != mock_eligible — must hold structurally, not merely by the accident of today's content having no `"gl"`-tagged rows. A Mock route that persists real results but draws from the general Practice pool is exactly the "future Mock development accidentally consuming ordinary Practice content" this decision exists to make impossible.
**Date:** 2026-08-16
**Implications:** No Mock content authored, no `mock_eligible` row created, no new Mock system built — this decision closes an existing architectural gap in existing routes only. Phase F (Mock Weekend) must still reconcile the broader duplication across all 5 routes identified above (only one is fully governed end-to-end); this decision does not perform that reconciliation, only the firewall correction.

### Decision 60 — Continuous Writing's uncalibrated AI score quarantined from mastery/readiness evidence
**Decision:** **AUTHORITATIVE SAFETY RULE:** an assessment value not validated/calibrated against an approved educational standard must not determine durable mastery or CSSE readiness. The full pathway was traced end-to-end (not assumed from the prior baseline report): `app/api/writing-feedback/route.ts` sends a learner's submission to OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini` and returns an `overallScore` (0-100) whose own system prompt states, in its own words, it "is not calibrated against any exam board's mark scheme and must not be described as one." `WRITING_CORRECTNESS_THRESHOLD = 70` (`lib/learningEngine/practiceContent.ts`) converts this into a boolean that two real call sites feed into the genuine Educational Intelligence evidence pipeline (`recordOutcome` → `applyAttemptOutcome`, `lib/ali/mastery.ts`) — the same pipeline Mathematics/English use, unmodified.
Answering the 10 questions directly, from code, not assumption:
1. **Presented to the learner?** Yes — `Overall score: {feedback.overallScore}/100`, with a disclaimer beneath it.
2. **Presented to the parent?** Not directly as a raw score; only indirectly, if evidence it produces surfaces on the same Readiness/Diagnostic components every competency shares (see 4-5).
3. **Affects mastery?** Yes, architecturally, prior to this fix — `recordAndAdvance`/`recordLegacyPracticeEvidence` omitted `supportTier`, defaulting to `"independent"`, which per `mastery.ts`'s own gate (`countsTowardMastery = isCorrect && supportTier === "independent"`) DOES count toward `distinctCorrectSessions` and CAN reach `"mastered"`.
4. **Affects readiness?** Yes, via the same competency (`WC-01`) that `readiness.ts`'s `ALL_ASSESSMENT_COMPONENTS` includes (Continuous Writing was not excluded like Applied Reasoning — it remains a real, current CSSE component; only its scoring is unreliable).
5. **Affects recommendations?** Only indirectly, via the same diagnostics `developmentAreas` mechanism every competency shares — no Writing-specific special-casing found.
6. **Affects admissions/readiness intelligence?** Only indirectly, via the same shared profile/competency pipeline — no separate defect found.
7. **Is it persisted?** The `overallScore` number itself is not persisted anywhere (ephemeral, computed per-request); only the derived `isCorrect` boolean is persisted, via `ali_student_question_history`.
8. **Downstream consumers?** `applyAttemptOutcome` (mastery), `processEvidenceForCompetency` (competency rollup → diagnostics/readiness/recommendations) — the same shared pipeline, no separate Writing-only consumer.
9. **Any UI claiming CSSE calibration?** No — the existing disclaimer already self-discloses non-calibration; not further changed here (no UI touched).
10. **Can it cause "mastered"?** **Confirmed yes, prior to this fix**, and via a real, currently-reachable path, precisely scoped: the live Practice pathway's Continuous Writing area has zero selectable content today (0 Writing rows `practice_eligible`; the one real row, `wrt-003`, is `provisional`) and throws before rendering, so that specific path is architecturally live but currently dormant. However, the separate legacy `/writing` route (`data/writing.ts`, reachable via the bottom-nav "Learn" item for any non-CSSE-pathway learner) serves a static prompt whose `id` (`"wrt-003"`) exactly matches that same real bank row. `recordLegacyPracticeEvidence` looks up a submitted `questionId` by ID only, with no `eligibility_status` check, so selecting that one specific prompt and requesting AI feedback **was a live, reachable, currently-exploitable path** to real `WC-01` evidence gated solely by the uncalibrated score, prior to this fix. The other 3 static prompts (`wrt-001`, `002`, `004`) have no matching bank row and were never at risk.
**Historical learner-data impact:** investigated, not assumed. `ali_student_question_history` returned `200` + `[]` for every query attempted (filtered to `wrt-*` question IDs, and completely unfiltered) via the anon key — including for Mathematics questions this same session's own 007L live-verification definitely practiced under a real profile. This is the same RLS-opacity pattern already established for `ali_family_review` (Decision 48) — **reported as a limitation, not asserted as "no historical impact."** No destructive remediation was performed on any existing row (none was attempted, and none would have been appropriate without genuine visibility into this table). If the Founder wants certainty, Level 1 evidence (direct Supabase Table Editor inspection, same standing as Decisions 51/53/54/56) would need to check `ali_student_question_history` for `question_id = 'wrt-003'`.
**Correction made (quarantine, not removal, per instruction):** `lib/learningEngine/legacyPracticeEvidence.ts`'s `LegacyPracticeAnswerParams` gained an optional `supportTier` field (default `"independent"`, preserving every existing caller — Maths/English/Vocabulary legacy pages — byte-for-byte, confirmed by source-level test). `app/writing/page.tsx` and `app/learning-intelligence/practice/[area]/page.tsx`'s Writing submit handlers now explicitly pass `supportTier: "supported"`. This reuses `mastery.ts`'s existing, already-proven gate (the exact same mechanism English/Maths guided attempts already rely on) rather than inventing new logic: a `"supported"` outcome still records genuine attempt/engagement evidence (`timesSeen`/`timesCorrect`/`lastAttemptCorrect` — the useful formative signal is preserved) but can never independently advance `distinctCorrectSessions` or reach `"mastered"`, and never revokes an already-established mastery state either. No AI feedback display, scoring model, or threshold was changed — the learner-visible experience is pixel-for-pixel identical; only the mastery-evidence consequence of that score is now safely bounded.
**Regression protection:** `tests/lib/learningEngine/writingMasterySafety.test.ts`, 7 tests — proves `applyAttemptOutcome` genuinely cannot reach `"mastered"` under `"supported"` across a repeated-correct pattern that would have (and, in a contrast test, does) reach it under the old `"independent"` default; proves attempt evidence is still recorded; proves an already-mastered state is neither promoted nor demoted; proves both real call sites in the codebase pass `"supported"`; proves the other 3 legacy callers are untouched.
**Rationale:** An unvalidated AI-generated number must never be able to tell a parent their child has durably "mastered" Continuous Writing — that is a real trust and safety risk on the product's central readiness claim, not a cosmetic concern. Quarantining via the existing supportTier gate is the smallest safe correction available: no new scoring model invented, no CSSE bands claimed, no historical data touched, no useful formative feedback removed.
**Date:** 2026-08-16
**Implications:** Continuous Writing remains architecturally NOT READY for a real scoring model (Decision 47's finding stands, unresolved) — this decision only closes the mastery-safety gap, it does not build the evidence-aligned assessment model Phase D (`ANGEL_CSSE_COMPLETION_PROGRAMME_V1.md` §6) describes. No Writing content authored. No production eligibility changed.

### Decision 61 — `ANGEL_CSSE_COMPLETION_PROGRAMME_V1.md` is the canonical CSSE completion baseline authority; its supply/count data is reproducible, not static
**Decision:** `ANGEL_CSSE_COMPLETION_PROGRAMME_V1.md` (created at baseline reconciliation, corrected and extended this Phase A session per Decisions 58-60) is confirmed as the **one canonical CSSE completion baseline and roadmap document** — no parallel or competing strategy document exists or is created by this Phase. `scripts/coverage-matrix.mjs` (new) regenerates every supply/count figure in the document's §3 Coverage Matrix (family row counts, Practice Eligible/Provisional splits, `addresses_misconception` population, and which families have real teaching-support code, cross-checked against `mathsTeachingContent.ts`/`guidedPractice.ts`/`adminReview.ts` directly) from live production on demand, so this data cannot silently drift into stale documentation the way the pre-Phase-A document's own Applied Reasoning framing had. Human-judgement columns (READY/PARTIAL/GAP classification, evidence-source citation, exam-preparation-maturity assessment) remain in the canonical document itself, deliberately not automated.
**Rationale:** A completion programme this large is only trustworthy if its numbers can be independently re-verified rather than taken on faith from a prior session's own report — the exact discipline this Phase A session itself was instructed to apply to the prior baseline report (Part 2: "Do NOT accept this finding... without verifying the actual current repository"), now extended forward so a future session can hold this document to the same standard.
**Date:** 2026-08-16
**Implications:** Future sessions should run `scripts/coverage-matrix.mjs` before treating any supply/count figure in `ANGEL_CSSE_COMPLETION_PROGRAMME_V1.md` as current, and should update the canonical document in place (never create a parallel one) when genuine drift is found. This decision does not itself change any content, eligibility, or Mock state.

### Decision 62 — Educational Increment 007M (CSSE Completion Programme Phase B, Mathematics Teaching Completion) implemented, tested, live-verified, and deployed; content review status recorded honestly, not inferred
**Decision:** The 007L Mathematics Teaching Architecture (MODEL / Guided step reveal / Independent / Remediation / Mastery evidence, `lib/learningEngine/mathsTeachingContent.ts`) is extended from the original 4 proof families to 22 further families — 26 of 27 named Practice Eligible Mathematics families now have real teaching content (up from 4, 15%, to 26, 96%). One family, `mr05-number-property-search`, is deliberately excluded (2 near-identical siblings, cannot demonstrate independent transfer — TRANSFER-UNSAFE). The 27-row legacy ungrouped pool (`family_id: null`) remains untouched and out of scope, per the directive's own instruction.
Full design (all 22 families' A-J teaching design), transfer classification, difficulty observations, and evidence pack recorded in `knowledge/.../ANGEL_PHASE_B_MATHEMATICS_TEACHING_COMPLETION_V1.md`. Implementation: commit `e3f1c09`, pushed to `origin/main`, deployed to production (Vercel READY, aliased `https://angel-11plus.vercel.app`).
**Automated verification:** `scripts/007l-model-verification.mjs` (extended from 4 to 26 families) — 26/26 families, 112/112 live rows, ALL CHECKS PASS, re-run fresh against production after deployment; 2 real MODEL/live-answer collisions caught by this script during design that manual fork review had missed (`mr02-nth-term`, `mr02-sum-difference`), corrected before implementation — the same "automated check catches what manual review misses" pattern 007L's own process demonstrated, working as intended. 35 unit tests (mathematical correctness independently re-derived per family), 8 mastery-protection tests (`lib/ali/mastery.ts` proven, not modified), 9 pre-existing Mock-firewall tests and 7 Writing-safety tests re-confirmed unregressed. Full suite: 307/307. TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard: caught and corrected 24 em-dash violations in newly-authored learner-facing text on first run, PASS (0 violations) after correction. Production build succeeds. Bank-wide Mathematics answer-validation regression (Decision 55): 168/168, unaffected. Production counts unchanged (TOTAL 264, PE 247, Maths PE 141, English PE 106, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0) — no eligibility migration.
**New architecture (bounded, reusing 007L's pattern, not a second engine):** `maxGuidedRevealSteps` on `MathsFamilyTeachingContent` caps Guided step reveal for families whose real, stored `workingSteps` end in a step that is just the final answer restated (`mr01-data-table`, capped at 1 of 2 real steps) or are entirely absent (`mr05-number-property`, 0, no reveal control renders at all) — found necessary by this increment's own family-by-family design research (not assumed safe in advance), confirmed live in production: the capped family's reveal button correctly read "0 of 1" (not the real 2) and disappeared after one reveal, never exposing the answer-bearing step before submission. Two new misconception categories added from convergent independent evidence across separately-researched families: `INCOMPLETE_REASONING` (`mr02-substitution`, `mr03-classify`), `PRECISION_INSTRUCTION_IGNORED` (`precision-frac`).
**Live learner-path verification (Decision 52's standing requirement):** performed via the real `/learning-intelligence/practice/mathematics` pathway, 2 sessions, 16 questions, a real profile. Directly walked: `mr04-best-value` (pre-existing, re-confirmed unregressed across both Guided and Independent modes, correct and incorrect), the legacy pool (firewall confirmed intact), `mr01-data-table` (the capped-reveal safety mechanism specifically confirmed working as designed), `mr05-constrained-multiple` (full MODEL/remediation cycle confirmed). The remaining 20 of 22 new families were **not** directly browser-verified this session — repeated tooling-level click failures (not a product defect, confirmed by retrying with multiple techniques) stopped further live sessions, and per this project's standing "avoid rabbit holes" discipline further retries were not pursued. This is recorded as a genuine, bounded gap in live-verification coverage, not glossed over — those 20 families rest on the automated script and unit-test evidence above, not on direct human observation.
**Content review status, recorded honestly (the key discipline this decision exists to establish before, not after, deployment — closing the exact gap Decision 57 had to close retroactively for 007L):** this content is implemented, automatically verified, and live-deployed, but **no Founder educational review of the authored MODEL/remediation content itself has occurred.** No family is claimed above **TEACHING PARTIAL / REVIEW READY** (007L's own maturity model). This decision explicitly does NOT claim TEACHING HUMAN APPROVED for any family — that requires the Founder's own review pack sign-off (Part 10 of the design document), not self-certification.
**Rationale:** Deploying before content review, as 007L itself did, is not being repeated as an unrecorded gap this time — the same real, live, tested code exists in production either way (matching the precedent that a technically-sound, safety-protected proof is a reasonable thing to ship for live verification), but the governance record now states plainly, at the same time as the deployment, exactly what has and has not been reviewed, rather than requiring a later session to discover and close the gap.
**Date:** 2026-08-16
**Implications:** Mathematics teaching-support maturity is now genuinely broad (26 of 27 families) but not yet Founder-content-approved for any of them. The next legitimate action on this specific work is Founder review of the design document's Part 3, not further Mathematics content expansion. No English, Writing, Mock, or difficulty-ladder work is authorised by this decision. Phase C (English Reading Completion) remains not started.

### Decision 63 — CSSE Completion Programme Phase B is educationally and technically CLOSED: Founder Mathematics Teaching Review of all 22 newly implemented families is complete, 22/22 APPROVED, closing the gap Decision 62 recorded honestly as outstanding

**Decision:** The Founder has completed independent educational review of all 22 Mathematics teaching families Educational Increment 007M added (Decision 62), via the `/admin-beta/review` "Mathematics Teaching Review" section (Educational Increment 007N, migration 059). This closes the exact gap Decision 62 recorded as outstanding: **no family may now be claimed above TEACHING PARTIAL/REVIEW READY without this step, and this step has now genuinely occurred.**

**Authoritative evidence source:** the Founder's own authenticated Supabase SQL Editor query against `public.ali_family_review` filtered to `review_type = 'maths_teaching_review'` (Level 1 evidence, same standing as Decisions 51/53/54/56 — this table remains RLS-opaque to every anon-key/script path in this repository, migration 054; confirmed blocked again this session, `200`+`[]` for an unfiltered `select` against the whole table, before the Founder's evidence was supplied). Reviewer: **Ayobami Lawal**. Qualification basis (recorded verbatim in each row's `notes`, per `buildNotesWithQualification`'s established convention): "Founder and parent with direct experience of preparing children for the Essex CSSE 11+, including experience of 11+ tuition, practice materials, mock examinations and supporting a child through successful grammar school preparation." Review date: 2026-08-16. `teaching_content_version` on every row: `Educational Increment 007M (CSSE Completion Programme Phase B), commit e3f1c09` — an exact match to `MATHS_TEACHING_CONTENT_VERSION` (`lib/adminReview.ts`) and to the commit that has been live in production, unmodified, since Decision 62, confirming every review judged the exact deployed teaching content, not a drifted or since-amended version.

**Decision breakdown: APPROVED = 22, APPROVED WITH AMENDMENT = 0, REQUIRES REVALIDATION = 0, REJECTED = 0, TOTAL = 22.** All 22 required family IDs are present and individually accounted for: `mr01-average-mean`, `mr01-data-table`, `mr02-compare`, `mr02-far-ratio-context`, `mr02-nth-term`, `mr02-sequence-rule`, `mr02-substitution`, `mr02-sum-difference`, `mr03-angle-sum`, `mr03-classify`, `mr03-coordinate`, `mr03-mixed-perimeter`, `mr04-compound-percentage`, `mr04-elapsed-time`, `mr04-far-percent`, `mr04-far-recipe`, `mr04-mixed-divisibility`, `mr05-constrained-multiple`, `mr05-factors-primes`, `mr05-number-property`, `precision-dec`, `precision-frac`. No amendment, revalidation, or rejection was recorded against any of them.

**CONTENT REVIEW is explicitly distinguished from MATHEMATICS TEACHING REVIEW, per this decision's own governing directive.** 16 of these 22 families already carried an earlier `review_type = 'content_review'` decision from the Pilot or Controlled Review Batches 2-4 (Decisions 51/53/54/56): `mr02-compare` (Pilot); `mr03-classify`, `mr04-far-percent`, `mr04-mixed-divisibility` (Batch 2); `mr01-average-mean`, `mr01-data-table`, `mr02-nth-term`, `mr03-coordinate`, `mr04-elapsed-time` (Batch 3); `mr02-far-ratio-context`, `mr02-sum-difference`, `mr03-mixed-perimeter`, `mr04-compound-percentage`, `mr04-far-recipe`, `mr05-constrained-multiple`, `mr05-factors-primes` (Batch 4). The remaining 6 (`mr02-sequence-rule`, `mr02-substitution`, `mr03-angle-sum`, `mr05-number-property`, `precision-dec`, `precision-frac`) had never been reviewed in any form before this decision. In every case, that earlier content-review approval (where one exists) judged only the underlying question wording/answer/CSSE-fit, and predates the MODEL/Guided/Remediation teaching layer this decision approves — it is never read as covering the teaching layer. This decision does not restate, merge with, or rely on those earlier rows; it stands on its own, distinct `review_type = 'maths_teaching_review'` evidence trail (migration 059), exactly as designed.

**Re-verification against the exact approved implementation (not merely re-asserted from Decision 62 — independently re-run this session):** full test suite 329/329 (307 pre-existing + 22 added for the review interface itself, Educational Increment 007N). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard: 0 violations. `scripts/007l-model-verification.mjs` re-run fresh against live production: 26/26 families (the 4 original 007L proof families plus the 22 now-approved Phase B families), 112/112 live rows, ALL CHECKS PASS, no MODEL/live-answer collision. `scripts/007k-bankwide-answer-regression.mjs` (Decision 55's own standing regression): 168/168, unaffected — unit-answer/exact-format protections remain intact. Production build succeeds. `mr05-number-property-search` independently reconfirmed excluded from both the teaching content (`getMathsTeachingContent` returns `undefined`) and the review target list (`MATHS_TEACHING_REVIEW_TARGET_IDS` — remains TRANSFER-UNSAFE, has no teaching support, and none is claimed). Mastery-protection tests (`lib/ali/mastery.ts`, unmodified) re-confirmed: a supported/Guided success cannot independently establish mastery or advance `distinctCorrectSessions`.

**Production counts, re-verified live via `scripts/coverage-matrix.mjs` this session: TOTAL 264, Practice Eligible 247, Maths PE 141, English PE 106, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — byte-identical to Decision 62's baseline. No eligibility migration performed by this decision or by the review process itself** (`submitMathsTeachingReview` only ever `INSERT`s an `ali_family_review` row; it has no `ali_question_bank` write path, by the same design `submitReview` has always followed).

**What this decision does NOT claim, stated explicitly per its own governing directive:** it does not claim Phase E (difficulty/transfer-depth expansion) is complete — 14 of the 22 approved families remain honestly classified TRANSFER-LIMITED (Phase B's own Part 3/7), and that classification is unchanged by Founder approval of the teaching content built on top of it; a Founder approving a family's teaching does not retroactively deepen its sibling variety. It does not claim `mr05-number-property-search` has teaching support; it remains deliberately excluded. It does not alter the 4 original 007L proof families' own, earlier, separate governance (Decision 57) — they are not re-reviewed or re-approved by this decision, only reconfirmed structurally intact (26/26 in the same live-verification script run, unchanged).

**Rationale:** Closes the exact gap Decision 62 named honestly at deployment time rather than glossing over it — content was live and technically sound, but Founder educational judgement, the one thing no automated check can substitute for (mathematical correctness, MODEL clarity, remediation fairness, transfer-classification fairness — Part 10 of the design document's own list), had not yet occurred. It now has, on the record, with named authoritative evidence, not inferred from a screenshot or an aggregate count alone (per this session's own explicit instruction not to infer approval from supplied screenshots — the individual per-family SQL evidence was required and obtained before this decision was written).

**Date:** 2026-08-16

**Implications:** **CSSE Completion Programme Phase B (Mathematics Teaching Completion) is CLOSED — both technically (Decision 62) and now educationally (this decision).** Mathematics teaching-support maturity stands at 26 of 27 named families, all Founder-approved; `mr05-number-property-search` and the 27-row legacy ungrouped pool remain out of scope, untouched. No difficulty-ladder (Phase E), Mock (Phase F), or English Reading Completion (Phase C) work is authorised by this decision. The next programme decision belongs to Founder/Product leadership, per the roadmap's own Phase B STOP condition (`ANGEL_CSSE_COMPLETION_PROGRAMME_V1.md` §10).

### Decision 64 — CSSE Completion Programme Phase C (English Teaching Completion and Learner Experience Assurance): full audit complete, one bounded remediation-rendering defect closed (Educational Increment 007O), English Teaching Review readiness built (Educational Increment 007P), Founder educational review outstanding

**Decision:** Phase C is authorised, scoped, and executed exactly as directed: a controlled completion increment for the 9 named English Reading/Comprehension families, explicitly excluding Continuous Writing, Mock, Phase E, mass content authoring, and any other pathway. Full findings recorded in `knowledge/.../ANGEL_PHASE_C_ENGLISH_TEACHING_COMPLETION_V1.md`.

**Production baseline re-reconciled, unchanged:** TOTAL 264, PE 247, Maths PE 141, English PE 106, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — no discrepancy from the directive's own stated baseline, confirmed both before and after this phase's changes.

**Defect found and closed (Educational Increment 007O, commit `7b69638`):** `addresses_misconception` was 100% populated for every named, Practice Eligible English question (human-reviewed, Batches 1-4) but never rendered in `ReadingActivity` — only `MathsActivity` received it. `wave1-fam-direct-retrieval` (14 PE, the largest named family) had zero wrong-answer remediation of any kind as a result. Fixed additively (never replaces the existing structural classification), gated identically to `MathsActivity`'s own pattern (`submitted && !lastCorrect`), framed as a general common-mistake note never a diagnosis of the specific learner. Live-verified in production across all 3 structurally distinct scoring paths (auto-verified `sequencing`, previously-zero-remediation `direct-retrieval`, self-assessed `two-character`) — confirmed working exactly as designed, no leakage, no internal terminology. 6 new regression tests (source-text assertions, mirroring the established `writingMasterySafety.test.ts` pattern — no `jsdom`/RTL exists in this project).

**Review readiness built (Educational Increment 007P, commit `3b48afd`):** a new "English Teaching Review" section in the existing `/admin-beta/review` architecture, covering the 8 learner-reachable named families (`wave1-fam-tick-justify` excluded: 0 Practice Eligible). Reuses the existing 18-criterion `REVIEW_CRITERIA`/`ReviewSubmission` shape unchanged (migration 047 already designed these broad enough for English) via a new, additive `review_type = 'english_teaching_review'` (migration 060) — structurally distinct from `content_review` so an earlier Batch 1/2 approval, which predates this phase's remediation-rendering change for several of these 8 families, can never be read as covering it. `ReviewForm` gained one minimal, additive `reviewType` prop (default `'content_review'`, every pre-existing content-review call site byte-for-byte unaffected).

**Genuine findings disclosed, not glossed over (full detail in the design document):** 4 of 9 named families have no MODEL (`direct-retrieval`, `synonym-battery`, `tick-justify`, `emotion-cause`); only 1 of 9 (`multiselect`) has an explicit directly-evidenced-vs-Angel-authored exam-strategy citation; only medium/hard difficulty values exist anywhere in the named estate (no `easy`, no designed ladder, mirroring the Mathematics finding); and — the most significant new finding — **15 of the 19 distinct English passages are shared across multiple families (typically 6-7 families per passage)**, and `groupingKeyOf()`'s fallback-to-passage-only-when-family-absent behaviour means **no passage-level exposure tracking exists for any named family**, in either the within-session diversity mechanism or the cross-session question-level cooldown. This is a real, evidenced anti-memorisation gap, not solved by this phase (fixing it would touch shared, subject-agnostic selection code Mathematics also depends on — explicitly out of this phase's bounded scope) and is recorded here for Founder/Product leadership to prioritise.

**Verification:** full suite 344/344 (329 at Phase B's close, +6 for 007O, +9 for 007P and its own tests). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard 0 violations (9 em-dash instances caught and corrected on first run). Production build succeeds both times. Bank-wide Mathematics answer-validation regression (Decision 55): 168/168, unaffected — this phase touches no Mathematics code, re-run anyway per standing discipline. Mastery/support regression unaffected (`lib/ali/mastery.ts` untouched). Production counts re-queried after both deployments: unchanged from baseline.

**Content review vs. teaching review, explicitly distinguished:** several of the 8 English Teaching Review targets already carry an earlier `content_review` decision from the Pilot or Controlled Review Batch 2 (Decisions 51/53) — that earlier approval judged the underlying question content and predates 007O's remediation-rendering change; it is never read as covering it. This is the same discipline Decisions 62-63 established for Mathematics, now applied to English.

**Migration 060 not yet applied to production** — same standing pattern as every migration in this project (RLS-opaque to the anon key on read either way; the write path requires the new `review_type` value the constraint must permit, confirmed not yet demonstrably applied). Requires Founder application via Supabase Dashboard SQL Editor before the English Teaching Review section can accept a submission.

**Rationale:** Mirrors Phase B's own precedent exactly (Decision 62 → 63's two-step pattern: technical completion recorded honestly as NOT educationally approved, then a genuine Founder review gate built and left for the Founder to use) — the Founder's own directive for this phase explicitly required exactly this ("technical completion is not educational approval").

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Implications:** Phase C is **technically complete and PASS WITH FINDINGS** (the genuine passage-exposure and MODEL-coverage gaps above, both disclosed, neither fixed, neither blocking the one bounded correction this phase authorised). Phase C is **NOT YET educationally complete** — Founder review of the English Teaching Review pack (8 families) remains outstanding, exactly mirroring the gap Decision 63 later closed for Mathematics. No Continuous Writing, Mock, Phase E, or other-pathway work is authorised by this decision. The next action is (a) Founder application of migration 060, then (b) Founder educational review of the 8 English Teaching Review targets — not Phase D/Mock/Phase E, which remain Founder/Product leadership's own decision per the governing directive's own explicit sequencing.

### Decision 65 — CSSE Completion Programme Phase C is educationally and technically CLOSED: Founder English Teaching Review of all 8 targeted families is complete, 8/8 APPROVED, closing the gap Decision 64 recorded honestly as outstanding — outstanding depth/breadth findings explicitly preserved, not falsely resolved

**Decision:** The Founder has completed independent educational review of all 8 English Teaching Review targets Educational Increment 007O's remediation-rendering change affected (Decision 64), via the `/admin-beta/review` "English Teaching Review" section (Educational Increment 007P, migration 060). This closes the exact gap Decision 64 recorded as outstanding: no family may be claimed as having Founder-approved teaching content without this step, and this step has now genuinely occurred, for these 8.

**Authoritative evidence source:** the Founder's own authenticated Supabase SQL Editor query against `public.ali_family_review` filtered to `review_type = 'english_teaching_review'` (Level 1 evidence, same standing as Decisions 51/53/54/56/63 — this table remains RLS-opaque to every anon-key/script path in this repository, migration 054; confirmed blocked again this session, `200`+`[]` for an unfiltered `select`, before the Founder's evidence was supplied). Qualification basis (recorded in each row's `notes`, per `buildNotesWithQualification`'s established convention, identical text on all 8 rows): "Founder and parent with direct experience of preparing children for the Essex CSSE 11+, including experience of 11+ tuition, practice materials, mock examinations and supporting a child through successful grammar school preparation." `teaching_content_version` on every row: `Educational Increment 007O (CSSE Completion Programme Phase C), commit 7b69638` — an exact match to `ENGLISH_TEACHING_CONTENT_VERSION` (`lib/adminReview.ts`) and to the commit that has been live in production, unmodified, since Decision 64, confirming every review judged the exact deployed remediation-rendering behaviour, not a drifted or since-amended version.

**Decision breakdown: APPROVED = 8, APPROVED WITH AMENDMENT = 0, REQUIRES REVALIDATION = 0, REJECTED = 0, TOTAL = 8.** All 8 required family IDs are present and individually accounted for: `wave1-fam-direct-retrieval`, `wave1-fam-emotion-cause`, `wave1-fam-quote-explain`, `wave1-fam-sequencing`, `wave1-fam-synonym-battery`, `wave1-fam-two-character`, `wave1-fam-vocab-explain`, `wave2-fam-multiselect`. `wave1-fam-tick-justify` is correctly absent — 0 Practice Eligible, not learner-reachable, never a target of this review (Decision 64's own scoping).

**Reviewer-name data-quality discrepancy, disclosed not corrected:** 7 of 8 rows record reviewer `Ayobami Lawal`; the `wave1-fam-two-character` row records `Ayobami Lawl` (missing the final "a"). Treated as the same reviewer identity, not a different one and not grounds to invalidate that row's educational decision, because every other field on that row — qualification basis, `teaching_content_version`, review date, decision — is identical to the other 7. **No established correction mechanism exists for this table**: `ali_family_review` has no `UPDATE`/`DELETE` RLS policy by design (migration 054's own comment: "This migration only ever allows INSERT... never UPDATE or DELETE"), so mutating the historical row is not merely undesirable but not possible through the application's own governed path, and this decision does not attempt it. Recorded here, transparently, as the safest available governance treatment, per this project's standing discipline of never silently rewriting historical evidence.

**CONTENT REVIEW is explicitly distinguished from ENGLISH TEACHING REVIEW, per Decision 64's own design intent.** Verified precisely against the real `PILOT_TARGET_IDS`/`BATCH2_TARGET_IDS` arrays (`app/admin-beta/review/page.tsx`): **all 8** of these families already carried an earlier `review_type = 'content_review'` decision — 5 from the Pilot (Decision 51: `wave2-fam-multiselect`, `wave1-fam-sequencing`, `wave1-fam-quote-explain`, `wave1-fam-two-character`, `wave1-fam-vocab-explain`) and 3 from Controlled Review Batch 2 (Decision 53: `wave1-fam-direct-retrieval`, `wave1-fam-synonym-battery`, `wave1-fam-emotion-cause`). Every one of those earlier approvals judged only the underlying question content and predates Educational Increment 007O's remediation-rendering change; none is read as covering it. This decision's approval is new, distinct, dated evidence for the teaching-layer change specifically, exactly as designed — unlike Mathematics Teaching Review (Decision 63), where 6 of 22 families had never been reviewed in any form before, every one of English's 8 targets already had a genuinely separate, earlier content-review history.

**Re-verification against the exact approved implementation, independently re-run this session:** full test suite 344/344, unchanged since Decision 64's own close. English-specific and regression suites re-run explicitly: `englishAnswerValidation.test.ts`, `englishErrorClassification.test.ts`, `guidedPractice.test.ts` (007G sequencing-anchor and 007H emotion-cause corrections both re-confirmed intact), `guidedSequencingCorrection.test.ts`, `englishRemediationRendering.test.ts`, `adminReview.test.ts` — 119/119. `exposureIntelligence.test.ts`, `mathsMasteryProtection.test.ts`, `englishPassageExposure.test.ts`, `englishWave2ExposureExpansion.test.ts`, `writingMasterySafety.test.ts` — 36/36 (mastery-protection and exposure/selection regression, both subject-agnostic and Mathematics-shared code, confirmed unaffected — this phase touches neither). Bank-wide Mathematics answer-validation regression (Decision 55): 168/168, unaffected. TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard: 0 violations. Production build succeeds. No answer leakage and no learner-facing internal ID/engine terminology were found live in production during Decision 64's own walkthrough (3 families, all 3 structurally distinct scoring paths) — the code has not changed since, confirmed by a clean `git diff` between that verification and this decision, so that evidence remains current, not re-walked live a second time this session.

No eligibility migration performed by this decision or by the review process itself (`submitEnglishTeachingReview` only ever `INSERT`s an `ali_family_review` row, no `ali_question_bank` write path, by the same design every prior review-submit function in this project has followed).

It now has, on the record, with named, per-family authoritative evidence, not inferred from the interface's own aggregate counter (per this session's own explicit instruction).

**Implications:** **CSSE Completion Programme Phase C (English Teaching Completion and Learner Experience Assurance) is CLOSED — both technically (Decision 64) and now educationally (this decision).** English remediation-rendering maturity stands at 9 of 9 named Practice Eligible families, the 8 materially affected by this phase all Founder-approved; `wave1-fam-tick-justify` and the 13-row legacy ungrouped pool remain out of scope, untouched. No Continuous Writing, Mock, Phase E, or other-pathway work is authorised by this decision. ### Decision 66 — CSSE Completion Programme Phase D (Continuous Writing Educational System, Assessment Standard and Readiness): full evidence/architecture/bounded-proof programme complete (Educational Increment 007Q); Founder educational review of the reflective/discursive task family outstanding

**Decision:** Phase D is executed exactly as scoped: a full evidence reconciliation, an explicit TEACHING/PRACTICE/ASSESSMENT-separated architecture, and one bounded proof implementation for the reflective/discursive task family, explicitly excluding Mock, Phase E, mass content authoring, and any other pathway. Full findings and design recorded in `knowledge/.../ANGEL_PHASE_D_CONTINUOUS_WRITING_STANDARD_V1.md`.

**Current-state estate, reconciled fresh:** two structurally separate Writing implementations (`app/writing/page.tsx`, legacy/gamified, reachable; `WritingActivity` inside the CSSE Practice pathway, currently unreachable — `ali_question_bank` has exactly 1 Writing row, `wrt-003`, `provisional`, 0 Practice Eligible), one shared AI feedback endpoint. **New finding, confirmed by direct query:** the one real DB Writing row's prompt is a byte-for-byte copy of the legacy static pool's own persuasive-speech prompt — not authored to match either real evidenced CSSE genre (reflective/discursive or picture-narrative). Not fixed this phase (a content-governance action, out of scope); disclosed.

**Evidence, re-verified against current official sources, not only the repository's own 2026-08-11 review:** fetched `csse.org.uk` directly (2026-08-17) — the official 2027 Entry Information Guide (`csse.org.uk/storage/2026/03/CSSE-Information-Guide-2027-Entry.pdf`) confirms English 60+10 min, Mathematics 60 min, 50/50 standardised weighting, no offer below 303, exactly matching prior repository findings, no conflict. The CSSE homepage's own words directly confirm Decision 58's Applied Reasoning exclusion ("With effect from September 2024 (2025 Entry) the English paper does not contain Applied Reasoning questions") — Decision 58's classification stands, now with stronger, dated, directly-quoted official corroboration than it had originally. **Resolved a standing repository evidence gap:** the official Continuous Writing sample mark scheme the repository already held as `CSSE-002` (previously classified "undated, cannot confirm currency") is confirmed, via the current live `csse.org.uk/examination/` page, to be the same document (dated 7 April 2020) and still the current, live-linked official rubric six years on.

**Architecture (design only, most of it not built this phase, per the bounded-proof instruction):** DIAGNOSE → TARGETED TEACHING → COMPONENT PRACTICE → GUIDED APPLICATION → COMPLETE WRITING → TIMED WRITING → ASSESSMENT → FEEDBACK → DELIBERATE IMPROVEMENT → REASSESSMENT → MASTERY MAINTENANCE. CSSE-evidenced 5-dimension rubric (Ideas / Vocabulary+Spelling / Grammar / Structure / Punctuation), kept visibly distinct from Angel's own design decisions (planning scaffolds, remediation categories, the Angel Progress Indicator) throughout.

**Bounded proof implemented, Educational Increment 007Q, commit `6853a43`:** MODEL + planning scaffold for `writing-reflective-discursive` (`lib/learningEngine/writingTeachingContent.ts`, the same `Record<family,content>` pattern Mathematics Phase B proved); a pre-flight confidence gate (`lib/learningEngine/writingRubric.ts`) replacing the prior arbitrary 60-word heuristic with the real CSSE-evidenced 6-sentence minimum, plus off-topic/template/prompt-injection detection; a rewritten `app/api/writing-feedback/route.ts` returning the 5-dimension rubric analytically, replacing the prior invented 6-quality craft list. **Decision 60's mastery quarantine is preserved unconditionally, not weakened or made confidence-dependent** — every AI-scored Writing attempt still records `supportTier: "supported"`, full stop, regardless of confidence or score.

**Genuine defects found and fixed via live calibration (`scripts/writing-rubric-calibration.mjs`, real OpenAI calls, not simulated), exactly Part 11's own intent:** (1) an `indexOf`-inside-a-loop bug undercounting sentences whenever a delimiter string repeated identically; (2) the model was observed to reliably omit `overallScore` from its own JSON output for some responses (`finish_reason: "stop"`, not a truncation — a genuine omission) — the prior `?? 0` fallback would have silently shown a fabricated "0/100" for a response the model never rated that low; fixed by never trusting the model's own score at all, computing it deterministically server-side from the five (validated) dimension levels instead, so it can never be missing or self-contradictory; (3) the off-topic heuristic's lack of stemming produced near-universal false positives (e.g. "patient" vs. "patience"), flagging even a live-tested "very strong" response as low-confidence on every run — fixed with a bounded `stem()` truncation, re-verified live afterward. A real prompt-injection attempt embedded in student writing was tested live and did not hijack the model's output.

**Review readiness built, mirroring Mathematics (Decision 62-63) and English (Decision 64-65):** a "Continuous Writing Teaching Review" section in `/admin-beta/review`, `review_type = 'writing_teaching_review'` (migration 061, not yet applied to production — same standing pattern as every migration in this project). Unlike the Mathematics/English sections, there is no live DB question to show (no content was authored this phase) — the pack presents the MODEL, planning scaffold, and rubric design directly.

**Verification:** full suite 380/380 (344 at Phase C's close, +36 new for this phase). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard 0 violations (4 caught and corrected on first run). No new eslint debt (`practice/[area]/page.tsx`'s own pre-existing baseline, 6 problems, independently re-confirmed identical before and after this phase's changes via a stash comparison). Production build succeeds. Bank-wide Mathematics regression 168/168, Mathematics Teaching Architecture live-verification 26/26 families, Mock Content Firewall 9/9 — all re-run and unaffected, this phase touches none of that code. **Live production regression check performed post-deployment** (not merely a calibration script): submitted a real response through the reachable `/writing` route on `https://angel-11plus.vercel.app`, confirmed the rewritten endpoint returns a genuine, non-zero, text-grounded result ("74% · Strong", specific strengths quoting the submitted text) — the `overallScore` defect found in calibration is confirmed fixed in live production, not just in the test script.

**Production counts, re-verified live via `scripts/coverage-matrix.mjs`: TOTAL 264, Practice Eligible 247, Maths PE 141, English PE 106, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0** — unchanged throughout. No content authored, no eligibility touched.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim the picture-narrative task family exists (deferred, needs a non-copyrighted image asset); it does not claim Continuous Writing content is ready for Practice (the one real DB row remains `provisional`, unreachable, and content-mismatched to real CSSE genre evidence, disclosed not fixed); it does not claim any evidence standard beyond `supportTier: "supported"` has been established for AI-scored Writing; it does not make any Mock-readiness or predictive claim of any kind (Part 13 of the design document names the explicit boundary and what further calibration/evidence would be required before one could be made).

**Rationale:** Mirrors the exact two-step precedent Decisions 62-63 and 64-65 both established: build and technically verify first, record honestly that Founder educational review has not yet occurred, and provide the review mechanism rather than self-certifying novel teaching/assessment content — here applied to a genuinely new subject area (Writing) and a materially higher-stakes design decision (a rewritten AI assessment architecture) than either prior phase touched.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Implications:** Phase D is **technically complete and PASS WITH FINDINGS** (the genre-mismatched `wrt-003` row and the deferred picture-narrative family, both disclosed, neither blocking the bounded proof this phase delivered). Phase D is **NOT YET educationally complete** — Founder review of the one Continuous Writing Teaching Review target remains outstanding. No Mock, Phase E, or other-pathway work is authorised by this decision. The next action is (a) Founder application of migration 061, then (b) Founder educational review of the `writing-reflective-discursive` target at `/admin-beta/review` — not Mock or Phase E, which remain Founder/Product leadership's own decision per the governing directive's explicit sequencing and its own STOP condition.

### Decision 68 — Educational Increment 007S: passage-aware exposure protection shipped for English Practice, closing the structural gap 007R identified (`ANGEL_007S_CONTENT_FOUNDATION_AND_EXPOSURE_ARCHITECTURE_V1.md`); QT-MR-01 and QT-RC-10 family architecture designed but not authored

**Decision:** Following Founder/Product approval of Educational Increment 007R's strategic baseline (≈472 objective-question target, ≈45-55 English passage target, Mock Option A approved, GL/CEM/ISEB/Independent remain deferred), Educational Increment 007S was authorised to resolve the two structural prerequisites 007R identified before any 472-estate authoring begins. Part (A), passage-level exposure architecture, was investigated, confirmed live (not merely re-asserted from 007R), and **implemented**: `lib/ali/exposureIntelligence.ts` gained `passageGroupingKeyOf()` and a generalised `computeFamilyExposure(..., keyFn)`; `lib/learningEngine/sessionGenerator.ts`'s `reduceFamilyClustering()` and `applyRetrievalPriority()` were generalised to accept a key function (default-preserving, byte-identical for every existing caller) and are now each run a **second time**, keyed by passage identity, inside `generatePersonalisedSession()`. Part (B), structural family architecture for the two zero-family Question Types (QT-MR-01 Direct Arithmetic, QT-RC-10 Effect-of-Language), was designed in full (family count, structural rules, variation dimensions, misconception sets, difficulty profiles, transfer classification, proposed sibling depth) but **no content was authored or activated**, per the directive's explicit scope.

**Root cause, re-derived from the live implementation this increment (not inference from 007R):** `groupingKeyOf()` (`familyId ?? learningUnitId`) has, since migration 030/031 gave every named English family its own `family_id`, never fallen back to `learningUnitId` (the real passage id) for any named-family row. Live production query confirmed 15 of the 19 shared passages each feed 5-7 *different* named families (e.g. `wave1-eng-kitemaker` feeds 7), meaning neither the within-session diversification pass nor the cross-session spaced-retrieval pass could ever detect that two selected questions from two different families exposed the learner to the same underlying text. A dedicated regression test (`passageAwareSelection.test.ts`, test 3b) proves the pre-fix single-pass mechanism genuinely could not catch this pattern — the gap was real, not hypothetical.

**Verification:** Full suite 389/389 (380 baseline + 9 new, 0 regressions). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 233 files). Production build succeeds. Mathematics bank-wide answer regression (live) 168/168, unaffected. Mastery protection suites 24/24, unaffected. Mock Content Firewall suite unaffected — confirmed by direct grep that the only production caller of the changed functions is `generatePersonalisedSession()` (Practice only); Mock routes still use the separate `fetchQuestionBank()` path and are structurally unreachable by this change. `passageGroupingKeyOf()` is explicitly scoped to `subject === "english"`, confirmed inert (byte-identical output) for Mathematics by two dedicated regression tests.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all code changes: TOTAL 264, Practice Eligible 247, Maths PE 141, English PE 106, Writing PE 0, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — byte-identical, unchanged.** No migration was run; no `ali_question_bank` row was written; the only production interaction performed was `SELECT` queries.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim QT-MR-01 or QT-RC-10 have any new content, family, or Practice-Eligible row (both remain at 0 dedicated families, exactly as 007R found); it does not claim the ≈472 objective-question target has changed materially (007S's own more granular QT-MR-01 family design revises the precise central estimate to ≈483, an order-of-magnitude-consistent refinement, not a replacement); it does not authorise any bulk authoring, Mock implementation, Mock Eligible marking, Writing activation, `wrt-003` change, or other-pathway expansion.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** Closes the first of the two structural prerequisites 007R itself named as blocking safe content expansion — English's most severe, most concretely quantified content-architecture gap (007R's own C- Anti-Memorisation grade) — via the same generalised, already-proven mechanism the codebase's own prior documentation (Educational Increment 007A) established as the intended pattern for extending exposure intelligence to a new dimension, not a new engine.

**Implications:** Practice sessions for English now diversify and spaced-retrieval-deprioritise by BOTH family and passage identity, closing a real, live, previously-undetectable repeated-passage-exposure gap for every one of the 15 shared passages. QT-MR-01 and QT-RC-10 remain structurally ungrouped in production (design proposals exist, nothing activated) — a future content-governance increment may classify the legacy QT-MR-01 pool into the 4 proposed families and begin QT-RC-10 authoring against the 2 proposed families, but that authoring is not authorised by this decision. The next programme activity, per 007S's own STOP condition, is Founder/Product review of this increment, including the revised ≈483 estate estimate and the QT-MR-01/QT-RC-10 family proposals — no further increment begins automatically.

No row's `eligibility_status` was changed by this decision. No row was marked `mock_eligible`.

**Verification:** Full suite 398/398 (389 baseline + 9 new — 8 in the new content-regression file, 1 elsewhere). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 233 files) — the guard's own `.ts`/`.tsx`-only scan does not reach the new `.mjs` content sources, so a dedicated dash-check was added directly to `007tBatch.test.ts`; it caught and this decision's own authoring fixed one real em-dash in an English `modelAnswer` field before commit, not glossed over. Anti-memorisation audit: Mathematics STRONG (0 genuine duplicate structures across all 4 families, confirmed via structural fingerprinting including a like/unlike-denominator signal that correctly distinguishes two fraction siblings a coarser check first, wrongly, flagged); English STRONG (highest pairwise question-similarity 0.28, 14/14 distinct misconception tags).

### Decision 71 — Migration 064 Review-Surface Reconciliation: root cause confirmed via code (hardcoded batch-array grouping had no 007T entry), a bounded UI fix applied (`app/admin-beta/review/page.tsx` only); migration-064 row existence and the 26-item backlog composition remain unverified pending authenticated evidence (`ANGEL_007T_MIGRATION_064_REVIEW_SURFACE_RECONCILIATION_V1.md`)

**Decision:** Following the Founder's application of migration 064 and live screenshots of `/admin-beta/review` showing no identifiable 007T section (only the existing Pilot/Batch 2/Batch 3/Batch 4 sections and a collapsed "Full Review Backlog (26 pending, outside this pilot)"), this reconciliation traced the live review UI code directly. **Root cause confirmed with certainty, independent of any data question:** `FullBacklogSection`'s own filter excludes only four hardcoded arrays (`PILOT_TARGET_IDS`, `BATCH2_TARGET_IDS`, `BATCH3_TARGET_IDS`, `BATCH4_TARGET_IDS`); no fifth array existed for 007T, so even a perfectly successful migration 064 would place its 11 targets, unlabelled, inside the generic backlog rather than a dedicated section. Scenarios C (records not inserted), D (ID mismatch), and E (another missing prerequisite) were each explicitly investigated and ruled out or found not required to explain the symptom; scenario B (grouping/classification gap) is confirmed; scenario A (records genuinely inside the collapsed backlog) remains consistent with, but not proven by, this code-level finding.

**A bounded correction was applied**, mirroring the existing Batch2/3/4 pattern exactly: three new named ID arrays (Mathematics x4, English Effect-of-Language x2, Passages x5) and one new `SevenTSection` component, showing a "007T Content Review" section with three sub-groups, each reusing the existing `TargetCard` and detail-view machinery unchanged — no new evidence-fetching code, no new review mechanism. The section carries 5 explicit disclosure lines (all provisional/no auto-activation, Mathematics teaching-content gap, English uses new Effect-of-Language families, passage review separate, no Mock involvement). Each sub-group renders an honest "not yet visible, confirm migration 064 applied" message if its targets are absent — the fix makes no claim about, and does not depend on, whether migration 064's rows actually exist. Only `app/admin-beta/review/page.tsx` was changed.

**What remains explicitly unverified, per this project's own RLS-evidence discipline (Decisions 48/53/54/56/63/65/67):** whether migration 064 actually inserted its 11 intended rows, and the exact composition of the 26-item backlog. `ali_family_review` is RLS-opaque to the anon key (re-confirmed live this session: `200`/`content-range: */0`/`[]`, the identical signature every prior instance of this wall has produced). Two exact authenticated SQL queries were provided for the Founder to run via Supabase Dashboard > SQL Editor, one targeted at the 11 expected IDs and one enumerating the full live pending set (which answers both questions at once). **No claim is made that migration 064 succeeded or failed** — that determination is deferred to the Founder's own authenticated evidence.

**Verification:** Full suite 398/398. TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 233 files — one em-dash in the new disclosure text found and fixed before commit). Production build succeeds. Bank-wide Mathematics regression 188/188. Passage-aware exposure (Decision 68) and Mock Content Firewall suites re-run explicitly, unaffected. Production counts re-queried live before and after all code changes: TOTAL 298, Practice Eligible 247, Maths PE 141, English PE 106, Provisional 51, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged throughout, matching the expected state exactly.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** A missing-section symptom has (at minimum) two independent candidate explanations — a data problem (migration 064 didn't insert) and a code problem (the UI can't display it even if it did) — and this project's own discipline requires tracing the real code and live data rather than guessing which. The code-level cause was fully resolvable from static inspection alone and is fixed; the data-level question is not resolvable without authenticated access this session does not hold, and is therefore left open and explicitly requested rather than assumed either way.

**Implications:** Once the Founder confirms (via the requested queries) that migration 064's 11 rows exist, they will render immediately and correctly in the new "007T Content Review" section with zero further code changes — the fix is already live in this commit. If the queries instead show 0 or partial rows, migration 064 itself needs re-examination (a data question, not a UI question, and out of this decision's scope). No family is approved, no content is activated, and no further increment begins by this decision.
