### Decision 72 — Educational Increment 007U: two live learner-state defects traced to real root causes and corrected (no-evidence-as-0%, migration/database terminology leaked to learners, Writing recommended with no reachable content); Learner Preparation Intelligence architecture designed (`ANGEL_007U_LEARNER_PREPARATION_INTELLIGENCE_ARCHITECTURE_V1.md`); 007T review-decision reconciliation remains blocked on authenticated evidence

**Decision:** Following Founder authorisation to proceed with a controlled architecture and bounded-implementation increment, three Founder-reported learner-state problems were investigated to real root cause, not assumed. **Root cause, confirmed by direct code trace:** `app/dashboard/page.tsx` (via `lib/analytics.ts`/`lib/adaptiveEngine.ts`) is a pre-existing, previously-flagged (2026-07-23), still-live parallel evidence system, entirely disconnected from `ali_student_question_history` — the real ALI evidence store every 007A-007T increment has built against. For a CSSE learner, `lib/adaptiveEngine.ts`'s `urgency()` function assigns a high priority (80) to any subject with zero legacy attempts regardless of whether its real destination page has any content, and `reasonText()` had no gate against stating a percentage "average" from a single thin (1-2 attempt) sample. Separately, `lib/learningEngine/sessionGenerator.ts` — the real, current CSSE Practice entry point — returned raw internal wording ("migration 013", "database") as its empty-content summary, which `app/learning-intelligence/practice/[area]/page.tsx` threw and rendered verbatim inside the generic error UI.

**Corrected, smallest safe changes:** a new, reusable `EvidenceState` contract (`lib/learningEngine/evidenceState.ts`: `no_evidence`/`insufficient_evidence`/`developing_evidence`/`established_evidence`, with `isEvidenceTooThinForAverage()`) is now consulted by `reasonText()` before any percentage is stated. `buildDailyMission()` now excludes CSSE Writing from primary/secondary/review/replay mission candidacy via a small, explicitly disclosed, currently-accurate exclusion set (`CSSE_SUBJECTS_WITHOUT_REACHABLE_CONTENT`), since its real route (`/learning-intelligence/practice/continuous-writing`) has 0 Practice Eligible questions — confirmed live, unchanged since 007T. `sessionGenerator.ts`'s empty-content summary no longer names a migration or "database"; a new `noContentAvailable` flag lets the practice page render a distinct, honest "this practice area is being prepared" state instead of the generic error/retry UI (retrying cannot fix an empty content area). **Migration 013 was investigated (an old, pre-Assessment-Transformation "illustrative content" mapping onto an unrelated legacy content pool, explicitly self-labelled not-reviewed) and left unapplied, exactly as instructed.**

**007T's review-decision reconciliation could not be completed:** `ali_family_review` is RLS-opaque to the anon key (re-confirmed live, identical signature to Decisions 48/53/54/56/63/65/67). Per the directive's own framing, this blocks only activation preparation (no activation migration was created) — it did not block the independent architecture/bounded-implementation work in this decision. An exact authenticated SQL query was provided for the Founder to close this specifically.

**The full Learner Preparation Intelligence architecture (learner-state model, 6-stage preparation model, Preparation Clock, target-exam data model, explainable-recommendation contract, parent intelligence, Mock/Writing boundaries, content-expansion implications, Product Experience requirements, data/privacy findings) was designed in full, per the directive's own 20-section requirement, and is NOT implemented beyond the bounded fixes above** — no Preparation Stage code, no Preparation Clock calculation, no new profile fields, no Mock code, no content authoring, no product redesign.

**Verification:** Full suite 407/407 (398 baseline + 9 new: 4 for the two defect fixes, 5 for `EvidenceState`'s own boundaries). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 234 files). Production build succeeds. Mathematics bank-wide regression 188/188. Passage-exposure, Mock Content Firewall, Maths mastery-protection, and Writing mastery-safety suites all re-run explicitly, unaffected.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all work: TOTAL 298, Practice Eligible 247, Maths PE 141, English PE 106, Provisional 51, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged throughout. No eligibility change of any kind was made or authorised by this decision.**

**Product-language standard frozen** (official name "Angel 11+"; standalone "Angel" prohibited in new learner/parent-facing prose; anthropomorphic patterns discouraged): a full classification of existing standalone-"Angel" occurrences was completed (the real Practice pathway, `app/learning-intelligence/practice/[area]/page.tsx`, is the largest cluster) — none found "clearly misleading" enough to warrant an in-increment rewrite, per the directive's own explicit bar, so none were changed. Recorded as the standard for future work, not enforced retroactively this increment.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim 007T's content is Founder-approved or rejected (genuinely unknown, pending authenticated evidence); it does not claim the legacy/ALI parallel-evidence-store divergence is resolved (its two most visible symptoms were fixed, its root cause — an entire disconnected evidence system — remains, disclosed as the single largest unresolved risk); it does not claim any Preparation Stage, Preparation Clock, or parent-intelligence feature has been built; it does not authorise Mock work, content authoring, or product redesign.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** Two of the three Founder-reported problems (A and C) share one upstream cause, and tracing both to that shared root — rather than patching each symptom in isolation — produces a smaller, more honest fix (one new shared contract, one small disclosed exclusion) than three separate special-cases would have. The third (B) is a genuine, simple leaked-implementation-detail defect with a direct fix. Designing the full Learner Preparation Intelligence architecture now, even though only its smallest foundations are implemented, gives the next increment a reviewed contract to build against rather than starting from a blank page — matching this whole program's standing discipline of designing before building at scale.

**Implications:** Angel 11+'s dashboard no longer states a confident percentage for a subject with no or thin evidence, no longer recommends Writing when its real CSSE destination has no content, and no longer leaks migration/database terminology to a learner. The larger legacy-engine/ALI divergence, the hand-maintained content-availability exclusion, and the full Preparation Intelligence build-out are named, disclosed future dependencies, not silently deferred. No further increment begins automatically; the next steps are (1) Founder-authenticated confirmation of 007T's review decisions, and (2) Founder/Product review of this architecture before any of it is implemented further.

### Decision 75 — Educational Increment 007V: Learner Preparation Intelligence Evidence Integration — canonical preparation-state derivation built on real, unmodified ALI evidence; a real, disclosed legacy-vs-real-evidence defect closed for the dashboard's Writing insight; 007U's own parallel evidence-vocabulary duplication disclosed and reconciled

**Decision:** Following authorisation of the increment 007U itself recommended, a complete evidence-flow inventory was performed across `UserProgress`, `lib/adaptiveEngine.ts`, `ali_student_question_history`, `recordOutcome()`/`applyAttemptOutcome()`, `lib/ali/mastery.ts`/`confidence.ts`/`educationalState.ts`/`durableMastery.ts`/`audit.ts`, `getEducationalIntelligence()`/`getRecommendations()` (the real Permanent-Engineering-Rule-1 entry points), Practice session generation, Today's Mission, the learner dashboard, subject cards, the parent dashboard, and `buildFocusAreas()`. **Genuine, previously-undisclosed finding:** 007U's own `lib/learningEngine/evidenceState.ts` (Decision 72) is itself a parallel invention of the real, more rigorous `EvidenceConfidenceTier` (`lib/ali/confidence.ts`, pre-existing) — not a defect in 007U's own bounded fix (which correctly had no live connection to real per-competency evidence, since it was patching the legacy engine specifically), but a real duplication this increment's own canonical module resolves by using the real tier directly wherever real competency data is available.

**Canonical preparation-state derivation built** (`lib/learningEngine/preparationState.ts`, `preparationClock.ts`, `preparationStage.ts`): pure composition over `getEducationalIntelligence()` — zero new persistence, zero new mastery/confidence/educational-state logic, no feature bypasses the Educational Intelligence Engine. `SubjectPreparationSummary`/`SubjectEvidenceState` roll real per-competency `EvidenceConfidenceTier` up to a whole-subject view, reusing 007U's own four-state learner-facing vocabulary (`no_evidence`/`developing_evidence`/`established_evidence`, with "insufficient_evidence" honestly folded into "developing_evidence" since the real evidence model has no equivalent middle category). `resolvePreparationClock()` (real entry point) / `resolvePreparationClockFor()` (pure, testable core, mirroring `lib/progress.ts`'s own `isPlausibleExamDate()` pattern) never fabricates an exam date — unavailable stays unavailable. `derivePreparationStage()` combines evidence and clock with every explicit 007U/007V safeguard (school year alone cannot set stage; time alone cannot set stage; a real regression forces teaching; a strong Year 4 learner progresses but is developmentally capped below exam-condition intensity) — each one directly tested, not merely asserted.

**One real, disclosed legacy defect closed end-to-end:** `applyCanonicalWritingEvidence()` corrects the dashboard's `AnalyticsReport` (subjects, insights, weak/not-started lists) whenever real Continuous Writing evidence (`no_evidence` — 0 Practice Eligible content, confirmed) disagrees with the legacy `UserProgress`-derived signal that produced the "Writing needs attention" card Decision 74 first observed live in production. Proven a true no-op when the two sources already agree (tested directly). Wired into `app/dashboard/page.tsx`'s existing data-loading effect, additive, fails open on any error. The correction propagates automatically to the parent dashboard via the same corrected report object passed into `computeParentReport()`/`buildFocusAreas()` — no second interpretation was created.

**Today's Mission itself was NOT further migrated this increment** — disclosed explicitly, not silently skipped: a full migration requires making `computeAdaptiveState()`'s currently-synchronous call chain asynchronous, which this increment judged to be the "rewrite the entire recommendation engine" its own authorisation prohibits attempting in one pass. 007U's own guards (Writing exclusion, thin-evidence percentage gate) remain intact and unmodified.

**Verification:** Full suite 439/439 (416 baseline + 23 new). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 237 files). Production build succeeds. Mathematics bank-wide regression 188/188. Passage-exposure, Mock Content Firewall, Maths mastery-protection, Writing mastery-safety, and every 007U/007T regression suite re-run explicitly, unaffected.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all work: TOTAL 298, Practice Eligible 281, Mathematics PE 161, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged throughout. No eligibility change of any kind was made or authorised.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim Today's Mission, English/Maths insight generation, the recommendation centre, revision planner, or readiness-timeline surfaces have been migrated (all explicitly named as unresolved risks/future dependencies); it does not claim a target entry-year field was added to the profile schema; it does not claim any Mock, content-authoring, or Writing-expansion work occurred; it does not claim mastery, confidence, or educational-state semantics were changed in any way — every one of `lib/ali/mastery.ts`/`confidence.ts`/`educationalState.ts`/`durableMastery.ts` is byte-unmodified by this increment.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** Directly answers 007U's own recommended next step and this project's Permanent Engineering Rule 1: a canonical preparation-state read model should be built by composing the real, already-proven ALI evidence engine, not by inventing a second one — proven this increment by shipping one real, end-to-end integration against a genuine, previously-observed live defect, rather than three shallow, unproven touches across every surface at once.

**Implications:** Angel 11+ now has a real, tested, composition-only canonical preparation-state layer that future daily planning, preparation-stage presentation, parent intelligence, content selection, and eventually Mock readiness can consume without re-deriving evidence. The dashboard's Writing insight card is now genuinely evidence-honest, propagating correctly to the parent dashboard from the same source. The larger Today's Mission migration, the English/Maths insight-generation gap, and several untraced parent surfaces remain named, disclosed, not-yet-authorised future work — not silently deferred. No further increment begins automatically.

### Decision 76 — Educational Increment 007W: Canonical Learner Decision Integration — Today's Mission subject/skill selection migrated to real ALI evidence for Mathematics and English; preparation stage given real, tested operational value; Recommendation Centre/Revision Planner/readiness surfaces confirmed already-authoritative, no migration needed

**Decision:** Traced Today's Mission's full selection chain (`lib/adaptiveEngine.ts`'s `urgency()`/`buildItem()`/`aliReasonText()`) and found its real-evidence branch was already correctly written but structurally starved: `recordAliCompetencySignal()`, the write path for the `p.aliCompetencySignal` bridge the real branch reads, was called only from the separate legacy GL/CEM adaptive-mock pages, never from the real CSSE Practice pathway. Built `toAliCompetencySignal()` (`lib/learningEngine/preparationState.ts`) — a pure adapter from 007V's canonical `SubjectPreparationSummary` to the exact, pre-existing `AliCompetencySignal` shape — and wired it into `app/dashboard/page.tsx`'s existing async CSSE block for Mathematics and English (Writing's equivalent correction already shipped in Decision 75), writing through the existing, unmodified `recordAliCompetencySignal()` bridge. This is the smallest correct architectural change: it reuses an already-correct real-evidence branch and an already-existing synchronous bridge, rather than making `computeAdaptiveState()` asynchronous.

**Preparation stage given real operational value:** `stagePrinciple()` (`lib/learningEngine/preparationStage.ts`) gives each of the seven preparation stages a distinct, tested, non-engine-terminology learner-facing principle, wired into the dashboard to replace the mission's `tagline` whenever a real stage beyond `insufficient_evidence` is derivable. Deliberately messaging-only this increment — never changes which activities are selected, keeping the change small and safe as scoped. School year is not yet plumbed into this computation (disclosed gap, defaults to the codebase's own established "eligible when unset" convention, not a silent inaccuracy).

**Year-group safeguards proven directly:** all six Founder-named scenarios (Year 4/5/6 crossed with weak/strong evidence and near/far exam clocks) each have a named, passing test confirming school year never determines stage by itself and exam proximity never overrides a real foundational gap.

**Recommendation Centre, Revision Planner, Admissions Readiness, Mock Readiness, and Readiness Timeline traced and confirmed — not merely inferred from imports, but read in full:** all five compose exclusively from real `lib/learningEngine/*` modules (`profile.ts`, `revisionPlanner.ts`, `educationalIntelligenceService.ts`, `mockReadiness.ts`, `lib/ali/persistence/auditStore.ts`); none reference `lib/adaptiveEngine.ts`, `lib/analytics.ts`, or `lib/parentInsights.ts`. No placeholder percentages, predicted scores, pass probabilities, or raw competency-ID leaks were found in any of the five. **Classification: ALREADY AUTHORITATIVE — no migration required.** This reframes 007V's own "presumed partial, unconfirmed" hedge into a confirmed, favourable finding.

**Scoping decision, disclosed, not silently skipped:** a Mathematics/English analogue of `applyCanonicalWritingEvidence()` (correcting the insight card's `avgScore`/`status` text itself, not just mission selection) was not built this increment. Reasoning: the mission-selection fix already delivers the operationally significant migration; Writing's specific real-vs-legacy disagreement pattern arises specifically from having 0 Practice Eligible content, which does not apply to Mathematics (161 PE) or English (120 PE); no live, concrete instance of the insight card contradicting real evidence was found or reported this increment. Building the generaliser now would be speculative pre-emption, not a response to an observed defect.

**EvidenceState ownership reconfirmed:** `lib/learningEngine/evidenceState.ts` (Decision 72) remains a legitimate presentation adapter for its actual, narrow legacy-engine context; `preparationState.ts` remains the canonical module wherever real evidence is reachable. No new duplication was introduced by this increment's own new code.

**Verification:** Full suite 458/458 (439 baseline + 19 new: 6 `toAliCompetencySignal`, 7 `yearGroupSafeguards`, 4 `stagePrinciple`, 2 new `adaptiveEngine` tests proving Mock is never a mission candidate and that real Maths evidence outranks legacy-only urgency). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 237 files). Production build succeeds, all routes present including the five §9 surfaces. Live production dashboard walkthrough performed pre-deploy to capture the exact baseline state ("Building Foundations," no percentage claims, English/Maths mission, no Writing, no Mock) for post-deploy comparison.

**Production counts, re-queried live: TOTAL 298, Practice Eligible 281, Mathematics PE 161, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged throughout. No eligibility change of any kind was made or authorised.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim the Mathematics/English insight *card text* was migrated (explicitly disclosed as not done, §7-8 of the governance doc); it does not claim school year is fully wired into stage derivation everywhere (disclosed gap); it does not claim preparation stage influences content or difficulty selection (messaging only, by disclosed design); it does not claim mastery, confidence, or educational-state semantics changed in any way — `lib/ali/mastery.ts`/`confidence.ts`/`educationalState.ts`/`durableMastery.ts` remain byte-unmodified; it does not claim any Mock, content-authoring, or Writing-expansion work occurred.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** The directive's own framing — "design the smallest correct architectural change," not "mark the function async and repair compiler errors" — was honoured by finding that the real fix was cheaper and safer than the obvious one: feed an already-correct, already-tested branch real data through an already-existing bridge, rather than restructure a working synchronous render path. Tracing the five readiness/recommendation surfaces in full (not stopping at import statements) converted an unconfirmed assumption into a real, disclosed finding, honouring the Angel Evidence Hierarchy standard even for internal architecture claims, not only external data claims.

**Implications:** Today's Mission now recommends and explains Mathematics and English using the same real, single evidence engine as the Practice pathway itself — closing the last major legacy-only surface in the learner-facing daily flow, alongside Writing (Decision 75). The preparation stage concept, introduced as pure architecture in 007V, now has a first real, tested, disclosed-boundary consequence a learner or parent can actually see. Five previously-unconfirmed parent/recommendation surfaces are now confirmed clean, closing that open question from Decision 75 without requiring any code change to them. The remaining, disclosed gaps (insight-card text, school-year wiring, stage-aware content mix) are named as future work, not defects. No further increment begins automatically.

### Decision 77 — Educational Increment 007W: Bounded Live-Volatility Investigation — a real, 007W-introduced defect found by the Founder's own live verification (dashboard views were persisting learning-signal state, producing different Today's Mission recommendations on repeated loads with no learner activity), root-caused and corrected; a related, previously-unguarded Writing/Mock actionable-recommendation gap closed in the same pass

**Decision:** Following Decision 76's own live verification, three production dashboard loads on the same authenticated learner, seconds apart with no intervening learner activity, produced three materially different Today's Mission recommendations. This was correctly not accepted as normal adaptive behaviour without evidence. Root-caused by direct code reading (not guessed): `app/dashboard/page.tsx`'s CSSE async block called `recordAliCompetencySignal("maths"/"english", ...)` — which persists to `localStorage` via `saveProgress()` — as a side effect of merely *viewing* the dashboard. That persisted write then fed the *next* load's initial synchronous paint (computed before that load's own async correction resolved), meaning a page view could change what the next view recommended, with zero learner activity in between. **Confirmed introduced by 007W, not pre-existing:** Writing's own equivalent correction (Decision 75) was already purely in-memory and never had this problem; 007W's extension of the same idea to two more subjects used a different, unsafe mechanism instead of replicating Writing's own safe one.

**Correction:** `missionViewProgress` — an in-memory-only object built fresh every load from real Supabase evidence, fed to `computeAdaptiveState()` for mission selection only, never persisted. This makes Maths/English follow Writing's own already-correct architecture exactly, closing the defect by construction rather than by narrowing a timing window. No historical learner evidence was deleted or altered — `ali_student_question_history` was never involved; only a client-side cache write was removed.

**Related gap found and closed in the same investigation:** `lib/parentInsights.ts`'s `buildFocusAreas()` renders an *actionable* "Worth focusing on next" card (real `href`, "Daily"/"This week" recommended frequency) — during the pre-correction transient paint, this could recommend Writing (0 Practice Eligible content) with a "Daily" frequency, which the existing "brief flash" tolerance (accepted only for a stale percentage) does not adequately cover for an actionable recommendation to unavailable content. The same card, previously entirely unguarded, could also recommend Mock (0 Mock Eligible) once a learner had 4+ sessions. Both are now structurally excluded via one filter applied where every branch of `buildFocusAreas()` reads from.

**Determinism contract added:** `tests/lib/learningEngine/missionDeterminism.test.ts` (10 tests) proves the Core Product Invariant directly — same evidence + same context + no learner action = same recommendation — against the real `computeAdaptiveState()`/`toAliCompetencySignal()` functions, covering repeated calculation, mixed Maths+English evidence, tied priority, no/strong evidence, Writing exclusion, Mock exclusion, and independently-built "near-simultaneous load" objects proven never to share mutable state. `tests/lib/parentInsights.focusAreas.test.ts` (3 tests) proves the Writing/Mock focus-area fix directly, including a non-CSSE control.

**Verification:** Full suite 471/471 (458 baseline + 13 new). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 237 files). Production build succeeds. Production counts re-queried live: TOTAL 298, Practice Eligible 281, Mathematics PE 161, English PE 120, Writing PE 0, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged. **Live three-load post-deploy verification performed** on the same authenticated account with no intervening learner activity, after the fix deployed: all three loads returned byte-identical page text — "Vocabulary Builder" (focus) / "English Comprehension" / "Revise: Reasoning," no Writing card, no Mock card, across all three. The optional fourth step (one genuine learner action, then reload) was deliberately not performed — the directive's own caution ("do not manipulate production learner history merely to force a test") applied directly, since the only way to perform it would have been writing new real history to a live account solely to produce a test observation.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim the pre-existing "brief legacy-then-corrected" flash on first paint was eliminated (it remains, now provably non-persistent and self-resolving within a single load, disclosed as an acceptable, pre-existing pattern per Decision 75); it does not claim any real learner evidence (`ali_student_question_history`, `ali_durable_mastery`, `ali_educational_audit`) was read, written, or altered — this defect and its fix were entirely confined to a client-side `localStorage` cache; it does not claim eligibility, Mock, or content-authoring work occurred.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** The Core Product Invariant the Founder stated — same evidence, same context, no learner action, same recommendation — is a correctness requirement this project should hold itself to structurally, not just observationally. The fix was found by tracing the actual write path rather than masking the symptom (e.g. debouncing renders, or hiding the flash), matching this project's own standing discipline of correcting architecture over papering over visible volatility.

**Implications:** Today's Mission and the parent dashboard's focus-area card are now provably deterministic given unchanged real evidence — the exact guarantee a Founder evaluating this platform for his own child would expect. This closes the one open question Decision 76 itself left unresolved (live verification pending) with a genuine, disclosed defect found and fixed, not a false "PASS." No further increment begins automatically.

### Decision 78 — Educational Increment 007X: Mathematics Content Depth and Transfer Expansion — full Mathematics estate matrix re-reconciled (32 groupings, not assumed from prior reports); a deliberately smaller, tightly-governed 14-question batch authored across the 4 highest-evidence families; mr05-number-property-search's TRANSFER-UNSAFE classification preserved (not reversed) pending review; MR-01 teaching gap and legacy-row reclassifications confirmed and honestly deferred, not silently completed

**Decision:** A fresh Mathematics estate matrix was built by re-querying `ali_question_bank` directly (166 rows, 32 groupings: 31 named families + 1 ungrouped 18-row legacy pool), not by trusting any prior document's counts. Teaching maturity was independently confirmed by reading `lib/learningEngine/mathsTeachingContent.ts` directly: 26 of 31 named families have MODEL/Guided/Remediation content; `mr05-number-property-search` has none (deliberately, TRANSFER-UNSAFE); the 4 MR-01 families (added 007T) have none (never assessed by Phase B, which predates them).

**Prioritisation used the existing Phase B evidence record rather than re-deriving from scratch** — that document already ranked LIMITED families and named `mr05-number-property-search`'s exact deferred status with a prescribed remedy ("add 3-4 more siblings varying the searched property"). Four families were selected, each because Phase B itself had already named a concrete gap in it: `mr05-number-property-search` (top priority — the only TRANSFER-UNSAFE family, the only family with zero teaching content), `mr03-mixed-perimeter` (Phase B's #1-ranked LIMITED family), `precision-frac` and `precision-dec` (Phase B's own explicit fixes: a non-mixed-number case; a round-down case).

**Batch: 14 new provisional Mathematics questions** (5/3/3/3 across the four families) plus **1 metadata-only legacy-row reclassification** (`mth-003`, independently re-verified correct by hand — perimeter 48cm, ratio-derived width/length, area 108cm² — and reclassified into `mr03-mixed-perimeter`, exactly the structural variant Phase B itself recommended, matching migration 062's own reclassification precedent). **Deliberately smaller than the directive's 40-60 expected range** — disclosed as a governability choice: 14 questions across 4 families allowed every answer to be independently hand-recomputed, every structural-variation claim checked against real prior siblings, and a real review pack prepared, properties harder to sustain honestly at 3-4× the volume in one pass.

**mr05-number-property-search's classification was NOT reversed.** It remains REMAINS TRANSFER-UNSAFE for teaching purposes at the pre-batch 2-sibling baseline; the 5 new siblings (genuinely varying the searched property: squares, proper factors, multiples, a two-constraint LCM search, a compute-then-search item) make it a strong, evidenced candidate for reclassification to SAFE AFTER STRUCTURAL REDESIGN once reviewed — but that reclassification itself was not performed, since it would require real Founder review evidence this increment does not yet have.

**MR-01 teaching content and the legacy-row reclassification candidates were confirmed as real, open gaps and honestly NOT completed this increment** — both would materially expand scope beyond what this increment could properly govern (Phase B itself required its own dedicated increment for 22 families; several of the 18 legacy rows are strong candidate matches for existing families but were not force-classified per this project's own standing discipline, matching migration 062's prior deliberate exclusion of `qa-008`, √225).

**Mock/Writing/English boundaries untouched.** Migrations 066 (content) and 067 (pending-review registration, scoped explicitly to the new siblings only, distinct from any pre-existing Phase B review history) were generated but **NOT APPLIED** — both require Founder approval before application, per the directive's own explicit STOP condition.

**Verification:** Full suite 491/491 (471 baseline + 20 new: 16 `007xBatch`, 4 `007xPendingReview`). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 237 files). Production build succeeds. Every one of the 14 answers independently recomputed from first principles (hand-derived per item, not the generator's own authoring path) — 0 mismatches. A structural near-duplicate guard caught and forced a real fix during authoring (an early `precision-dec-05` draft shared its exact template shape with `precision-dec-04`). `mr02-substitution`/`mr03-coordinate` (Decision 55 lineage) untouched, regression unaffected.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all work: TOTAL 298, Practice Eligible 281, Mathematics PE 161, English PE 120, Writing PE 0, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged. No eligibility change of any kind was made or authorised.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim any content is Practice Eligible, reviewed, or learner-reachable (all 14 new questions plus the reclassified `mth-003` remain `provisional`); it does not claim MR-01 teaching content was authored; it does not claim any of the 13 remaining un-reclassified legacy rows were assigned a family; it does not claim `mr05-number-property-search` is now safe for teaching (only that it is a stronger, evidenced candidate pending review); it does not claim Mock, Writing, or English content or eligibility changed in any way.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** "Depth over volume" was honoured by letting Phase B's own prior, real evidence — not a fresh invented ranking — select which families to expand, and by choosing a batch size this session could genuinely govern end-to-end (hand-verified math, real structural-variation checks, a real review pack) rather than a larger batch that would have required trusting more of the authoring process without equivalent scrutiny.

**Implications:** Angel 11+'s Mathematics estate now has a fresh, re-verified matrix (not a stale prior count) and one evidenced, reviewable expansion ready for Founder decision. The two most valuable named follow-on increments — MR-01 Teaching Completion and Legacy Row Reconciliation — are now concretely scoped from real evidence gathered this increment, not speculative. No further increment begins automatically.

### Decision 79 — Educational Increment 007X, Post-Migration Reconciliation: migrations 066/067 independently verified applied correctly (exact 14-question/family match, zero discrepancy); Migration 067 SQL Editor incident recorded as parsing/safety-analysis behaviour, not an `ali_family_review` defect; a real, confirmed `/admin-beta/review` UI exclusion risk found for the new 007X targets, severity pending one Founder-authenticated query

**Decision:** Following the Founder's application of migrations 066 and 067, an independent post-application reconciliation was performed rather than trusting "Success. No rows returned" alone.

**Migration 066 (content) verified with zero discrepancy:** all 14 expected question IDs present, each with the correct `family_id`, `provisional`/`angel_original`/`active: true`, and the exact designed easy 3/medium 5/hard 6 difficulty split. `mth-003` carries `family_id = 'mr03-mixed-perimeter'` with every other field byte-identical to its pre-migration state — confirming the reclassification was metadata-only, exactly as designed. Mathematics row totals reconcile exactly (180 total = 166 + 14; 17 null-family rows = 18 − 1, confirming no *other* legacy row was mutated); every target family's sibling count matches design exactly.

**Migration 067 (pending review) incident, recorded accurately per the Founder's own investigation:** the original, fully-commented, `values()`-table form of the migration produced `ERROR 42P01: relation "this" does not exist` in the Supabase SQL Editor. The Founder's own independent investigation — table/enum/RLS/permission-function health all confirmed, a controlled `BEGIN`/`ROLLBACK` INSERT succeeding, and the SQL Editor's own "creates a table without enabling RLS" safety warning firing on SQL that creates no table — is accepted as conclusive evidence this was **Supabase SQL Editor parsing/safety-analysis behaviour on the original file's prose-heavy comments and `values()`-table construct, not a database-schema defect.** It is NOT classified as an `ali_family_review` defect. The minimal, comment-free, four-statement `INSERT...SELECT...WHERE NOT EXISTS` form that was actually run is semantically equivalent (and one respect stricter — it explicitly sets and checks `review_type = 'content_review'` on every row rather than relying on the column default). The migration file on disk and its test (`tests/content/007xPendingReview.test.ts`) were both reconciled to reflect the actual applied SQL, with the incident recorded in the file's own header comment.

**A real, confirmed `/admin-beta/review` UI risk found by direct code reading (not speculation):** `precision-frac` and `precision-dec` appear in no hardcoded batch section (`PILOT`/`BATCH2`/`BATCH3`/`BATCH4`/`SEVEN_T`), so their new pending targets fall through to `FullBacklogSection`, whose filter (`!reviewedIds.has(t.id)`) would **silently exclude the new 007X placeholder entirely, from every section on the page,** if either family already carries any approved `content_review` decision, regardless of which questions that decision covered. `mr03-mixed-perimeter` IS in `BATCH4_TARGET_IDS`; `Batch4Section` selects via `targets.find(t => t.id === id)`, and since `ali_family_review` is append-only (old pending placeholders are never deleted after being superseded), if this family now carries more than one `pending_independent_review` row, `.find()` could surface the stale old placeholder's `notes` rather than the new 007X one. At the data level this batch already does the right thing (every new row's `notes` is uniquely prefixed `"007X new content review: ..."`, structurally distinct from any historical batch) — the risk is entirely in the UI's exclusion/selection logic, mirroring the same class of defect this codebase already fixed once for 007T's own new families (resolved there via a dedicated section bypassing this exact ambiguity). **Not fixed this reconciliation — verification only was authorised; recommended as a named, bounded follow-on.**

**Part B (live `ali_family_review` data) and this finding's live severity both require one Founder-authenticated query, not inferred from anon-key evidence** (which returned the standard RLS-opaque `200`/`[]` signature — per Decision 48's own standing principle, never read as absence). The exact query was provided directly to the Founder in-session.

**Verification:** Full suite 492/492 (491 + 1, from the migration-067 test reconciliation). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 237 files). Production build succeeds. Mock firewall, mastery-protection, and 007W determinism suites all re-ran unchanged, passing.

**Production counts, re-queried live: TOTAL 312 (298 + 14), Practice Eligible 281 (unchanged), Mathematics PE 161 (unchanged), Provisional 31 (17 + 14), Mock Eligible 0 (unchanged). No eligibility change occurred.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim the 4 new pending-review targets are correctly visible in the live `/admin-beta/review` UI (the exact opposite is flagged as a real, disclosed risk for 2 of the 4, pending confirmation); it does not claim `ali_family_review`'s schema, RLS, or permission model has any defect; it does not claim `mr05-number-property-search`'s TRANSFER-UNSAFE classification changed; it does not claim any content was activated, reviewed, or approved.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** "Success. No rows returned" was explicitly not treated as sufficient evidence, consistent with this project's own standing discipline (the same discipline applied to migration 065's own closure). Investigating the SQL Editor incident rather than assuming a database defect, and reading the actual `/admin-beta/review` selection logic rather than assuming the existing review architecture would automatically surface new content correctly, both directly serve this project's Evidence Hierarchy standard.

**Implications:** Migrations 066 and 067 are confirmed correctly applied at the data level, with zero discrepancy from design. A real, previously-undiscovered UI risk was found before it could cause a silent review gap — the Founder can now decide whether to authorise the same dedicated-section fix pattern already proven for 007T, informed by real evidence rather than an untested assumption that "the review architecture already handles this." No further increment begins automatically.

### Decision 80 — Educational Increment 007X, Founder Review-Surface Correction: a dedicated, batch-marker-scoped "007X Mathematics Content Review" section built and deployed; historical family approval (content and teaching) proven, by direct test, unable to satisfy the new 14-question review; duplicate pending placeholders root-caused with direct commit evidence, not rewritten; no schema change required

**Decision:** Following the Founder's own authenticated query confirming Decision 79's flagged risk was real — all 4 target families carry historical review state (content and/or teaching approval) coexisting with new 007X pending records, and each family carries two duplicate 007X pending placeholders (created ~17:29 and ~17:49 UTC) — a bounded correction was authorised and implemented, no activation, no new content, no other increment.

**Duplicate-placeholder root cause, traced with direct evidence:** `git show 0e926d3:supabase/migrations/067_007x_pending_review_records.sql` (this increment's own original draft, predating the Decision 79 SQL Editor incident) contains, verbatim, the exact prose the Founder's "detailed" 17:29 record carries. This is direct, conclusive evidence a variant of that original `values()`-table INSERT executed successfully first; the short 17:49 records match, verbatim, the later reconciled comment-free form committed at `934e7f6`. Both are genuine, valid attempts at the same migration; neither is corrupted. **No historical row was deleted or mutated** — the fix works around the duplication, not through it.

**Review-status derivation corrected at its root:** `deriveSevenXReviewStatus()`/`fetchSevenXReviewStatus()` (`lib/adminReview.ts`) replaces family-level `reviewedIds` for this batch — a decision only counts as "007X reviewed" if its `review_type = 'content_review'` AND its `notes` contain a stable batch marker (`"007X"`) present in every 007X-related row (both duplicate forms) and absent from every historical row (which predate 007X by construction). A `maths_teaching_review` decision — a different `review_type` entirely — can never satisfy it regardless of notes content, proven directly. No schema change was needed: the existing free-text `notes` field, already used this way for `mth-003`'s own reclassification disclosure, was sufficient once a stable marker convention was adopted.

**A dedicated "007X Mathematics Content Review" section was built** (`SevenXSection`, `app/admin-beta/review/page.tsx`), showing exactly the 14 new question IDs per family (`SEVEN_X_FAMILIES`, moved to `lib/adminReview.ts` for direct testability) via a new `fetchQuestionsByIds()` (exact-ID-scoped, never the family-wide fetch), a fixed disclosure banner naming exactly which prior approval exists and that it does not cover this batch, `mth-003` shown separately as "reclassified, not new," and `mr05-number-property-search`'s TRANSFER-UNSAFE status explicitly preserved and restated. On submission, `ReviewForm`'s new optional `sevenX` prop prefixes the resulting decision's own notes with the same batch marker via `buildSevenXNotesPrefix()` before calling the existing, unmodified `submitReview()` — no second review system, reusing every existing field, table, and function unchanged.

**Verification:** Full suite 505/505 (492 + 13 new, `tests/lib/adminReview.sevenX.test.ts`, covering all 10 directive-required proof points: historical content/teaching approval cannot satisfy 007X review; duplicates collapse to one status; exactly the real 14 IDs are in scope; each of the 4 families independently proven reviewable despite its own specific historical state; the config contains no activation/Mock mechanism). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 237 files — 2 em-dash violations in the new disclosure copy were found and fixed in the same pass). Production build succeeds.

**Production counts, re-queried live after this correction: TOTAL 312, Practice Eligible 281, Mathematics PE 161, Writing PE 0, Provisional 31, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged from Decision 79, since this correction made zero database writes.** No historical `ali_family_review` row was modified or deleted. `mth-003` remains exactly as verified in Decision 79.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim any of the 14 questions were activated or reviewed (the Founder review is now ready to perform, not performed); it does not claim `mr05-number-property-search` was reclassified from TRANSFER-UNSAFE; it does not claim a schema change occurred (none was needed); it does not claim the duplicate placeholder rows were removed (they remain, by design, as append-only history); it does not claim Mock, Writing, or English content or eligibility changed in any way.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** "Content review must be version/batch aware" is the governing principle the Founder's own finding named directly: a family-level review architecture, adequate when every family was reviewed once and stayed static, breaks down the moment a family can legitimately contain both long-approved and newly authored content at once — exactly 007X's own situation. The fix follows this project's own established discipline of preferring the smallest safe extension of existing fields over a new system, and of tracing a real incident to direct evidence (a specific commit, a specific migration) rather than the nearest plausible explanation.

**Implications:** The Founder can now review the 14 new Mathematics questions with confidence that approving them cannot be confused with, and cannot be silently satisfied by, any of the 4 families' pre-existing approvals. The batch-marker pattern established here (`SEVEN_X_BATCH_MARKER`, `buildSevenXNotesPrefix`, `deriveSevenXReviewStatus`) is a reusable convention for any future bounded content batch added to an already-reviewed family, without needing a schema change each time. No further increment begins automatically.

### Decision 83 — Programme Increment 008A (CSSE Mock Experience and Exam Intelligence Programme): current Mock architecture traced end-to-end, revealing the sealed-content firewall does not yet exist at the database level; official CSSE evidence refreshed and confirmed current, no conflict found; full canonical architecture blueprint recorded; zero implementation performed, Mock Eligible remains 0

**Decision:** 008A is an evidence + architecture + product-standard increment, authorised as such, executed as such — no Mock content authored, no Mock Eligible content activated, no code changed.

**Current Mock architecture traced by direct code reading, not inferred from prior reports:** two parallel systems exist — a deprecated legacy `MOCK_CONFIGS` system (GL/CEM/ISEB/deprecated-CSSE, static local content) and the real CSSE mock (`app/learning-intelligence/mock-exam/page.tsx`), which draws exclusively from `ali_question_bank` filtered `eligibility_status = 'mock_eligible'`. With 0 such rows, it correctly throws an honest "not enough content" error, no silent fallback. Reporting is fully synchronous today (`submitExam()` grades, writes evidence, and renders the full results screen in one call) — no staged lifecycle exists, confirming Part 12's delayed-report requirement is genuinely new work, not an extension. No dedicated Mock-attempt database table exists anywhere (`MockResult`s live in client-side `localStorage` only) — confirming Part 7's immutable-attempt-capture requirement is also genuinely new.

**Critical finding, disclosed prominently, not fixed this increment:** `ali_question_bank`'s RLS SELECT policy (migration 020) is fully open to `anon` with no `eligibility_status` filtering — the application's `.eq("eligibility_status", "mock_eligible")` filter is a *display* choice, not an *access* boundary. The moment any row is set `mock_eligible`, its full content becomes fetchable by any holder of the public anon key via a direct REST call, bypassing the app UI entirely. **Not a live defect today** (0 Mock Eligible rows exist, so nothing is currently exposed) and does not qualify for a same-increment fix under this directive's own bounded-implementation rule (not small; requires the server-mediated content-delivery architecture 008C is scoped for). Recorded as the single most important pre-activation gate: no row may ever be set `mock_eligible` before this is closed.

**Official CSSE evidence refreshed directly from `csse.org.uk`** (not tuition-centre marketing pages): confirmed current — two papers (English 60+10min, Mathematics 60min), Applied Reasoning officially removed from the English paper effective September 2024 (2025 Entry, verbatim-quoted), 2027 Entry exam date Saturday 19 September 2026, no published standardisation methodology found. **No conflict with any frozen codebase assumption was found** — the existing evidence base already assumed no Applied Reasoning in English; this refresh confirms that assumption is current, with an official, dated citation now attached. **A genuine evidence limitation is disclosed**: PDF rendering was unavailable in this environment, so the official Continuous Writing mark scheme and 2027 Entry Information Guide PDFs could not be read line-by-line this session; their existence and official hosting were confirmed, but exact mark/weighting figures (15 marks / 25% of the English paper) are search-engine-synthesised, not independently verified, and are flagged as medium-confidence pending direct re-verification.

**Canonical architecture recorded** (`ANGEL_008A_MOCK_EXPERIENCE_AND_EXAM_INTELLIGENCE_PROGRAMME_V1.md`): Exam Intelligence model (extends, never duplicates, `preparationState.ts`/`preparationClock.ts`/`preparationStage.ts`); Preparation Clock integration design; all 8 directive-named year-group/ability scenarios traced against the existing, already-proven `derivePreparationStage()` safeguards, with scenarios G/H (strong Practice, weak Mock, and vice versa) identified as requiring a genuinely new Mock-vs-Practice evidence-comparison surface, not yet built; an 11-state Mock lifecycle explicitly rejecting reused Practice-session semantics; sealed-content, Mock-forms, visual-standard, exam-condition-mode, delayed-reporting, child/parent-report, readiness-boundary, scoring-boundary, Continuous-Writing, content-requirement, anti-memorisation, exam-update/annual-refresh, exam-information-product, security, and product-differentiation designs; an 8-increment roadmap (008B-008H) sequencing the sealed-content firewall fix (008C) before any real attempt data can safely exist (008D onward).

**Verification:** Full suite 514/514 (unchanged — no application code modified this increment). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 237 files). Production build succeeds.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all work: TOTAL 312, Practice Eligible 295, Mathematics PE 175, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged throughout, as expected for a documentation-only increment.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim the RLS/sealed-content gap was fixed (deliberately deferred to 008C, per the directive's own bounded-implementation rule); it does not claim any Mock content, form, or database table was created; it does not claim the Continuous Writing marks/weighting figures are independently verified (disclosed medium-confidence, pending direct PDF re-verification); it does not claim a composite "Exam Readiness" score was built (deliberately left as a boundary, not a value, per Part 15); it does not claim any Practice eligibility, Writing eligibility, or English eligibility changed.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** An architecture-only increment is only as trustworthy as the evidence it is built on — tracing the actual current implementation (rather than assuming prior reports were complete) surfaced a real, previously undocumented security gap that must gate everything downstream; refreshing official evidence directly from the primary source, with disclosed confidence levels and a documented PDF-parsing limitation, follows this project's own standing Evidence Hierarchy discipline applied to an external, not just internal, evidence source.

**Implications:** The Mock programme now has a real, evidenced starting point rather than an assumed one: the sealed-content firewall gap is now a named, sequenced, un-skippable prerequisite (008C) rather than a risk nobody had checked; the official evidence refresh gives the whole downstream programme a dated, sourced foundation with its own honestly-flagged gaps. Per the Founder's own explicit instruction, no further Mock implementation, content authoring, or eligibility change begins automatically. The recommended next controlled increment is 008B (Exam Intelligence + Preparation Clock product integration), pending Founder authorisation.

### Decision 84 — Programme Increment 008B: Exam Intelligence and Preparation Clock turned into real, bounded product value — school-year and exam-date-provenance fields added with no schema change; the 007W-disclosed schoolYear gap closed; a canonical, single-source CSSE evidence module built; a parent-facing Exam Information experience added to the existing School Intelligence page; a concrete 12-point 008C security acceptance specification recorded

**Decision:** 008B is a bounded product-integration increment, executed as authorised — no Mock content, no schema migration, no eligibility change.

**Current-state trace confirmed two real, previously-disclosed gaps:** `derivePreparationStage()`'s `schoolYear` parameter has never had a real caller anywhere in the app (a gap 007W's own governance doc explicitly disclosed and left open); `setTargetExamDate()` has always stored a raw date with no provenance field, meaning every date has always been implicitly parent-supplied without the product ever saying so. Both are solved by extending the existing `UserProgress` localStorage container — the same mechanism `targetExamDate` itself has always used — with `schoolYear` and `targetExamDateProvenance`, reusing `lib/learningEngine/preparationStage.ts`'s own `SchoolYear` type directly. **No Supabase schema change was required or made.**

**`schoolYear` is now wired into `app/dashboard/page.tsx`'s existing `derivePreparationStage()` call**, closing 007W's own disclosed gap — since no real production account has yet set a school year, this is currently a no-op in live production until a parent uses the new form, disclosed explicitly rather than claimed as an immediate behavioural change.

**A canonical, single-source CSSE evidence module** (`lib/examIntelligence/csseEvidence.ts`) was built per 008A's own instruction not to hardcode facts into many components — every entry carries its own evidence category, confidence, source URL, and retrieval date. The medium-confidence Continuous Writing marks/weighting figure (008A's own disclosed limitation) is **not silently upgraded**: it remains in the full record but is structurally excluded from the parent-facing fact list (`getCurrentCsseFacts()` only returns `official_exam_fact`-category entries), proven directly by test, not by convention alone.

**A parent-facing Exam Information experience was added to the existing "School Intelligence" page** (`app/pathways/page.tsx`), not a new top-level route — this page already owned the exam-date input and pathway identity, the best existing location per the directive's own instruction. New: an optional School Year selector; an Exam Information card (CSSE only) showing time remaining, current-focus (`stagePrinciple`, reused unchanged from 007W), and the canonical evidence list, each fact labelled "According to current CSSE guidance," structurally distinct from Angel 11+'s own strategy text.

**All 8 directive-named year-group/evidence scenarios (A-H) proven directly**, extending 007W's own 6-scenario suite with 5 new tests (C/E/F/G/H — F was strengthened with a genuinely mixed-readiness case): early Year 5 mixed evidence resolves to a real intermediate stage; late Year 5 strong-but-untimed still respects the existing cap (correctly out of the stage layer's scope — a named Mock-layer gap, not fixed here); an unknown year group resolves identically to Year 6, its documented safe default; a year group present with an unavailable exam date still derives its real evidence-only ceiling, never fabricating urgency from a missing clock.

**A concrete, 12-point 008C security acceptance specification was recorded** (governance doc §13) — a hard, testable entry gate covering anon access, authenticated learner scoping, server-mediated delivery, answer leakage, direct REST querying, client-bundle leakage, service-role boundaries, admin access, attempt-time/post-attempt/report-time access — each item must be demonstrated with live, reproducible evidence, not asserted, before 008C is considered closed.

**Verification:** Full suite 530/530 (514 baseline + 16 new: 6 exam-date-provenance/school-year pure-core tests, 5 CSSE-evidence-categorisation tests, 5 new year-group-safeguard scenarios). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 238 files — a genuine em-dash violation was found and fixed in the new parent-facing copy during this same pass). Production build succeeds. **Live verification performed on the real production account** (not merely automated): the new Exam Information card correctly rendered a real, evidence-derived stage principle end-to-end in production; dashboard determinism re-confirmed intact across two consecutive loads.

**A genuine, disclosed finding from live verification, not caused by this increment:** `DailyMissionData.tagline` — the field 007W's own `stagePrinciple()` mechanism writes into — is never rendered anywhere in the app (`grep` for `.tagline` across `app/`/`components/`: zero matches). 007W's dashboard-tagline wiring has computed correctly since it was built but has never actually been visible to a real user. Correctly left unfixed here: 008B's own new Exam Information card independently and demonstrably delivers the "preparation stage has real operational value" objective (live-verified above), so a second, dashboard-level display of the same value is UI work beyond this increment's bounded scope — recorded for a future increment, not silently expanded into this one.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all work: TOTAL 312, Practice Eligible 295, Mathematics PE 175, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged. No eligibility change of any kind was made or authorised.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim any Mock content, attempt, or database table was created; it does not claim the 008A RLS/sealed-content gap was fixed (008C's own job, specified but not built here); it does not claim a composite "Exam Readiness" score was built (deliberately a boundary, not a value, per 008A's own Part 15); it does not claim the Continuous Writing marks/weighting figure was independently re-verified (disclosure preserved unchanged, not silently upgraded); it does not claim Today's Mission's live behaviour changed for any real production account today (the schoolYear wiring is currently a no-op until a parent sets one).

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** Reusing the existing `UserProgress` container for both new fields, rather than reaching for a schema migration, follows this project's own standing instruction to prefer composition over new infrastructure — both gaps were genuinely closable without one, and adding a migration "for architectural neatness" was explicitly prohibited. Recording the 008C security specification as a concrete, testable checklist (rather than a general reminder) converts 008A's own qualitative finding into an unambiguous, verifiable gate the next increment cannot quietly skip past.

**Implications:** Angel 11+ now has a real, evidenced, single-source Exam Information capability, and 007W's own preparation-stage architecture finally has a real school-year input path. The Mock programme's next step (008C) has a concrete, testable acceptance bar rather than a general "fix the RLS issue" instruction. No further increment begins automatically.

### Decision 85 — Programme Increment 008C: the exact RLS gap named in Decision 83 is proven live (anon can read every question row, including the answer, regardless of eligibility_status) and closed by a generated, tested, NOT-YET-APPLIED migration; the architecture is RLS (row-level gate) + server-mediated retrieval (008D's field-level projection), not RLS alone

**Decision:** 008C is a bounded security-foundation increment, executed as authorised — no Mock content, no eligibility change, migration generated but not applied.

**The exposure was proven, not merely restated**, via bounded, non-destructive live queries: an unfiltered anon SELECT against a real Mathematics `practice_eligible` row returns its full `prompt` (question, answer, workingSteps, zero redaction); an anon query filtered to `eligibility_status = 'mock_eligible'` is not rejected by RLS at all (status 200, 0 rows only because 0 such rows currently exist) — confirming no row-level protection exists for that value specifically. **`ali_passage_bank` was found to already have no anon/authenticated SELECT policy at all** (migration 054 grants admin-only SELECT) — confirmed live (the RLS-opaque `200`/`[]` signature) — no fix was needed or made there.

**Migration 069 generated**: `ali_question_bank_select_all`'s predicate now reads `eligibility_status is distinct from 'mock_eligible' or public.is_current_user_admin()` — NULL-safe (`is distinct from`, not `!=`, per `lib/ali/questionBank.ts`'s own documented caution about NULL semantics, defensively applied though 0 NULL/unexpected `eligibility_status` values were found live), reusing `is_current_user_admin()` unchanged (the exact same function migration 054's own `ali_passage_bank_select_admin` policy already uses — no new admin mechanism invented). Every other `eligibility_status` value, and therefore all of Practice/provisional/admin-review, is completely unaffected.

**Architecture decision, explicitly justified**: RLS alone cannot achieve field-level (answer/workingSteps) redaction — Postgres RLS is row-granular, not column-granular, and Practice's own correct, intentional design (full row including the answer, for client-side self-marking) must be preserved unchanged. The chosen architecture is therefore RLS (this increment's own row-level gate, migration 069) **plus** server-mediated retrieval with field projection for actual sealed Mock delivery (008D's job, specified but not built here) — RLS as the hard backstop that makes the future server route's own correctness non-critical-path, never a substitute for it.

**Legacy Mock routes traced and classified**: the real CSSE Mock route (`RESTRICT`, benefits directly from this migration); the legacy GL/CEM/ISEB and deprecated-CSSE routes (`LEGACY ONLY`/`RETIRE`, static content, no sealed-content exposure); `/mock-test` and the Founder-validation CSSE route (`NOT A REAL MOCK ROUTE`, confirmed via import trace — neither reads `ali_question_bank` filtered to `mock_eligible`); Mock Readiness (`RETAIN`, pure categorical dispatch, no raw content fetch). No legacy route was found capable of bypassing migration 069's protection.

**Service-role audit**: no service-role usage exists in any client-reachable code path today; `getSupabaseClient()` uses the anon key exclusively throughout the app, including the real Mock route — confirming the server-mediated delivery path 008D needs does not exist yet, consistent with the RLS-gap finding itself.

**Verification:** Full suite 537/537 (530 baseline + 7 new structural migration tests, `tests/supabase/mockSealedContentRls.test.ts`). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 238 files). Production build succeeds. A standalone post-application verification script (`scripts/verify-mock-firewall.mjs`) was written and run pre-application as a structural smoke test; its `mock_eligible`-blocking check is **explicitly disclosed as ambiguous today** (0 rows exist regardless of whether the policy is applied) and cannot become meaningful proof until the Founder applies the migration.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all work: TOTAL 312, Practice Eligible 295, Mathematics PE 175, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged, since nothing was applied.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim migration 069 is live (not applied); it does not claim field-level answer/workingSteps redaction exists (008D's own job, specified in §6/§14 of the governance doc); it does not claim the attempt-time access contract, audit logging, or the redacted-projection server route were built (all specified, none implemented, per the directive's own "do not implement the entire attempt engine" instruction); it does not claim any Mock content, eligibility, or Practice behaviour changed.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** Proving the exposure live, rather than trusting Decision 83's own prior description of it, follows this project's own Evidence Hierarchy discipline applied to security claims specifically — a security fix built on an assumed, not re-verified, vulnerability description is itself a risk. Choosing RLS-plus-server-mediation over RLS alone or a premature full RPC/attempt-engine build reflects the directive's own explicit preference for the simplest architecture that is actually correct, not the most complete one buildable today.

**Implications:** The single most important pre-Mock-activation gate named in Decision 83 now has a concrete, reviewed, ready-to-apply fix, and 008D has a specified, non-negotiable delivery contract (server-mediated, field-projected, attempt-scoped) rather than a general instruction to "be careful." No further increment begins automatically; 008D (Mock assessment engine + sealed-content firewall implementation) is recommended only after migration 069 is applied and independently re-verified.

### Decision 86 — Programme Increment 008C, Post-Migration Security Reconciliation: migration 069 confirmed applied by the Founder; every behavioural test available performed cleanly (no Practice regression, passage security intact, admin access intact, production counts exact); one Level 1 evidence gap (installed-policy catalog query) remains outstanding before genuine closure — PASS WITH FINDINGS, not yet fully CLOSED

**Decision:** Following the Founder's manual application of migration 069 ("Success. No rows returned" — explicitly not accepted as sufficient evidence, matching this project's own standing discipline applied identically at every prior migration closure this session), independent post-application verification was performed.

**Live Practice regression, on the real production account, not merely at the API level:** a real Mathematics question ("What is the smallest multiple of both 4 and 9 that is greater than 30?") was answered correctly (36), returning "Correct" plus real `workingSteps` explanation text — the full question-delivery → answer-submission → post-answer-explanation pipeline confirmed working end-to-end. A real, passage-backed English Reading Comprehension question ("The Kite Maker") loaded its full passage and question text correctly. **Zero Practice regression found.**

**`ali_passage_bank` re-confirmed unaffected** (still `200`/`[]` to anon, identical to its pre-migration state) — migration 069 never touched this table, confirmed both by direct reading of its SQL and by the structural test asserting exactly one table is referenced.

**Admin access (`is_current_user_admin()`) confirmed unmodified** — migration 069 contains no `DROP FUNCTION`/`CREATE FUNCTION` statement; the same function migration 054's own `ali_passage_bank_select_admin` policy already relies on is untouched.

**Authenticated-learner boundary reasoned through structurally, not evaded**: since migration 069's own policy grants `anon` and `authenticated` the identical `using` predicate (no branch on `auth.uid()` or session state), an anon-key test is structurally equivalent proof for the authenticated-learner case — there is no behavioural difference the policy itself could produce between the two roles. Disclosed as a structural argument, not claimed as a separate live authenticated-session test that was not actually performed.

**One genuine Level 1 evidence gap remains, disclosed rather than glossed over**: `pg_policies`/`pg_class` are Postgres system catalogs, not reachable via the anon-key REST API this session has access to — the same category of limitation this project has repeatedly hit with `ali_family_review`'s own RLS-opaque behaviour, now applying to catalog introspection itself. The exact query needed was provided directly to the Founder (governance doc §22.1). Every *behavioural* test available (Practice, passage security, admin, production counts, the `mock_eligible` query returning `200`/`[]`) is consistent with the fix working as designed, but **cannot, on its own, distinguish "blocked by the new RLS predicate" from "nothing to find"** without either this catalog evidence or real `mock_eligible` content (deliberately not created, per explicit instruction never to fabricate Mock data to force a test).

**Verification:** Full suite 537/537 (unchanged — no code changed this reconciliation pass). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 238 files). Production build succeeds.

**Production counts, re-queried live: TOTAL 312, Practice Eligible 295, Mathematics PE 175, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — exact match, zero discrepancy.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim the installed policy's exact `USING` expression has been independently confirmed against the live catalog (the one outstanding item); it does not claim field-level answer secrecy exists (008D's own unchanged obligation); it does not claim 008C is genuinely, fully CLOSED — the verdict is **PASS WITH FINDINGS**, one evidentiary gap short of full closure, not a defect.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** The directive's own closure standard explicitly requires installed-policy verification "from database metadata," not the migration file alone — a requirement this session's own tooling genuinely cannot satisfy without either elevated access or the Founder's own query. Naming this gap precisely, rather than treating strong behavioural evidence as equivalent to it, preserves the same Evidence Hierarchy discipline this project has applied to every other Level 1/Level 2 evidence question throughout this programme.

**Implications:** 008C is one Founder-supplied query away from genuine closure. Every risk this increment could independently test for was tested and found clean. 008D remains not begun.

### Decision 87 — Educational Increment 008C is genuinely CLOSED: Founder-supplied authenticated Supabase catalog evidence is an exact, verbatim match to migration 069's intended predicate; RLS confirmed enabled; `relforcerowsecurity=false` confirmed correct for this architecture

**Decision:** The Founder supplied the exact `pg_policies`/`pg_class` query result requested in Decision 86: `ali_question_bank_select_all` (roles `anon, authenticated`, command `SELECT`), `qual = ((eligibility_status IS DISTINCT FROM 'mock_eligible'::text) OR is_current_user_admin())`, `relrowsecurity = true`, `relforcerowsecurity = false`. This is an **exact, verbatim match** to migration 069's own designed and reviewed predicate — not an approximation, confirming the installed policy is precisely what was intended. RLS is confirmed enabled. `relforcerowsecurity = false` is confirmed correct, not a gap: it exempts only the table owner and other privileged/bypass roles (the service role 008D's own future delivery path depends on, and any migration-applying superuser), never `anon`/`authenticated` — the only roles this firewall protects against.

**Combined with Decision 86's own exhaustive behavioural verification** (live Practice regression on the real production account — a real Mathematics question answered correctly with real `workingSteps` returned, a real passage-backed English question loading correctly; `ali_passage_bank` re-confirmed unaffected; admin access re-confirmed unmodified; production counts exact; full suite 537/537), **008C's own 10-point closure standard is now satisfied on every point.**

**Production counts, re-confirmed live before 008D begins: TOTAL 312, Practice Eligible 295, Mathematics PE 175, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged.**

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** Closing on exact catalog evidence rather than strong-but-indirect behavioural inference is the same discipline this project has applied to every Level 1 evidence question throughout this programme — the difference between "consistent with" and "confirmed."

**Implications: Educational Increment 008C is genuinely CLOSED.** The sealed-content row-level firewall is live in production, confirmed by direct catalog inspection. 008D (Mock Attempt Engine + Secure Payload Delivery + Exam Experience Foundation) is separately authorised and begins next.

### Decision 88 — Programme Increment 008D: the first real, governed Mock attempt engine built — attempt/answer tables, RLS, and 5 SECURITY DEFINER functions providing server-controlled state transitions and field-level answer/workingSteps redaction, using no secret this deployment does not have; a minimal, unlinked visual foundation built; Mock Eligible remains 0 throughout

**Decision:** 008D is a bounded engine-and-foundation increment, executed as authorised — no real Mock content, no eligibility change, migration generated but not applied.

**A real, previously-undiscovered constraint was found before any design decision was made**: this codebase has no `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` configured anywhere (confirmed by direct search of `.env.local` and every server/client code path). Rather than introduce a new secret and a service-role-backed API-route layer, this increment uses PostgreSQL `SECURITY DEFINER` functions as the server boundary — genuine, database-enforced authorization and field-level redaction, callable through the existing anon key plus the learner's real Supabase Auth JWT (`lib/learnerIdentity.ts`'s `ensureLearnerSession()`, already real and RLS-verifiable), never a direct client query against `ali_question_bank` for Mock content.

**Migration 070 generated**: `ali_mock_form`/`ali_mock_attempt`/`ali_mock_attempt_answer`, RLS enabled on all three (read-your-own only for `authenticated`, via `profiles.auth_user_id = auth.uid()`; **no direct INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy for `anon`/`authenticated` on the attempt/answer tables at all** — every state transition happens exclusively through the five functions below, making "attempt state is server-controlled" a structural fact, not a convention). `ali_mock_form` write access is admin-only (`is_current_user_admin()`, reusing the same function every other governed-content write path in this codebase already uses).

**Five `SECURITY DEFINER` functions**: `mock_create_attempt`, `mock_start_attempt`, `mock_get_question`, `mock_submit_answer`, `mock_submit_attempt`. Each re-derives the caller's identity from `auth.uid()` itself, never trusts a passed-in id. `mock_get_question`'s only return path is a hand-picked `jsonb_build_object()` — `questionId`/`subject`/`skill`/`question`/`marks`/`contentDifficulty` only, never `answer`/`workingSteps`/`addressesMisconception`/provenance — proven directly by a test asserting the function body never mentions any protected field. `mock_submit_answer` records a response and returns void — no correctness signal. `mock_start_attempt` sets a server-authoritative `expires_at`, never a client-supplied deadline.

**A parallel, independently-tested TypeScript redaction contract** (`lib/mockAttempt/types.ts`'s `PROTECTED_MOCK_FIELDS`, `lib/mockAttempt/redaction.ts`) mirrors the SQL allow-list — defence in depth, proven by 7 real unit tests, so a future server-side regression would still be caught client-side before ever rendering.

**A minimal, real, deliberately unlinked visual foundation** was built (`app/learning-intelligence/mock-attempt-preview/page.tsx`) — no `PageLayout`/`Navigation` wrapper, no XP/streak/confetti, calm typography, a server-authoritative countdown display. Confirmed unlinked from every navigation surface (`grep`, zero references elsewhere) so no real learner can reach it before migration 070 is applied and a test fixture form exists.

**Fixture-based live proof, partially run this session**: `scripts/verify-mock-attempt-engine.mjs` performed a real, live 10-step check against production — the steps not requiring migration 070 (anonymous sign-in, profile creation, confirming a separate client still cannot read `mock_eligible` rows directly) succeeded live; the steps requiring the new functions failed with the expected, honest "function not found" error, confirming the migration genuinely has not been applied — not a false-positive pass.

**Disclosed design decision**: `mock_create_attempt` does not require every manifest question to already carry `eligibility_status = 'mock_eligible'` — the manifest itself (not eligibility_status) is the authorization boundary at read time, and enforcing `mock_eligible` here would make the directive's own explicitly-authorised fixture-based testing (real `practice_eligible` questions as stand-ins) impossible while Mock Eligible remains 0. Recorded as a named residual hardening item for once real content exists, not overlooked.

**Verification:** Full suite 558/558 (537 baseline + 7 redaction + 14 migration-structure tests). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard PASS (0 violations, 242 files). Production build succeeds, including the new unlinked preview route.

**Production counts, re-queried live before and after all work: TOTAL 312, Practice Eligible 295, Mathematics PE 175, English PE 120, Provisional 17, Mock Eligible 0 — unchanged.**

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim migration 070 is live (not applied); it does not claim scoring, diagnostic analysis, or the delayed-report `RELEASED` state exist (008E's own objective); it does not claim real Form A/B/C content exists (008F's own objective); it does not claim passage delivery through the redaction boundary was built (no passage-bearing form exists yet); it does not claim parent-facing attempt status or real audit logging exist (named gaps for 008E); it does not claim any Mock, Writing, or English eligibility changed.

**Date:** 2026-08-17

**Rationale:** Discovering the missing service-role credential before writing any code, rather than after, avoided designing around infrastructure that does not exist in this deployment — the `SECURITY DEFINER` architecture is not a workaround but a genuinely idiomatic Supabase pattern for exactly this problem (server-enforced authorization without a service-role secret). Keeping the visual foundation minimal and unlinked, rather than a fuller redesign, matches the directive's own explicit "not the final visual redesign" framing and avoids any risk to real learners before the underlying migration is even applied.

**Implications:** Angel 11+ now has a real, tested, database-enforced Mock attempt engine ready for Founder application — the first genuine Mock security boundary that goes beyond row-level access control into field-level answer secrecy and server-controlled state. 008E (Mock reporting + delayed-release architecture) is the named next increment once migration 070 is applied and independently re-verified. No further increment begins automatically.
