### Decision 106 — Governance record reconciliation (Stage 2 authorised conversationally, not persisted at Decision 105) + Stage 2 Educational Integrity Correction: a proven English assessment-validity defect (self-assessed correctness indistinguishable from Angel's own automatic verification, at the Evidence Confidence / Learning Engine V1 layers, in two separate parallel evidence-rollup systems) is corrected; a second, independently-discovered automatic false-positive defect (character-substring matching in `checkAcceptedAnswerSet`, Tiers 2/4/5) and a third (the legacy English heuristic's unconditional half-marks floor) are also corrected; own automated verification performed and PASSES; real-device/browser verification remains outstanding

**Governance record reconciliation (Part 1, first):** Decision 105's own text states Stage 2 (the Practice question-shell/keyboard rewrite) was "explicitly reserved and not begun" and that "no Stage 2 begin until the Founder has reviewed the rendered result directly," with Founder visual propagation approval recorded as PENDING. **This entry does not rewrite Decision 105** — it stood as written and its own record of that session's state is accurate as of when it was made. What is recorded here, for the first time, is a **subsequent, separate governance event**: the Founder authorised Stage 2 work conversationally, after Decision 105 was already written, in a later session — an authorisation that was acted on (real Stage 2 implementation work exists in the repository, self-labelled "Stage 2" in its own code comments — `lib/learningEngine/practiceInteractionGuard.ts`) but was never itself persisted into this log at the time it was given. This is a genuine, disclosed governance-record gap, consistent with this log's own established practice (Decision 92, 102, 103) of recording process anomalies plainly rather than smoothing them into the surrounding narrative as if they had always been in order. It is not being treated as a permission blocker (the Founder has now confirmed the authorisation directly, in this session), and it does not require the underlying Stage 2 implementation to be undone, re-justified, or re-verified — only that the record now honestly reflects when each approval actually happened.

**This decision's own new authorisation:** the Founder separately and explicitly authorises, in this session, the Stage 2 Educational Integrity Correction described below. **Stage 3 remains prohibited until Stage 2 is verified and closed** — this correction does not close Stage 2 (see Verdict).

---

**Background — the incident.** Founder real-use testing of English Comprehension Practice submitted the deliberately inadequate answer "hu" to `w1-raceday-05` ("Find two quotations that show Ade feels increasingly uncomfortable by the end of the passage, and explain what each one shows.", `TIER3_QUOTATION_PLUS_EXPLANATION`). The session reported "1 of 8 correct," yet the resulting Learning Report showed "Inference and Justified Interpretation: DEVELOPING." A prior checkpoint this session traced the exact mechanism and reproduced it with real, unmodified production code (not simulated): `w1-raceday-05`'s DB `skill` column is `QT-RC-02` → RC-02 = "Inference and Justified Interpretation" — an exact match to the Founder's own screenshot. Tier 3's scorer correctly never auto-verifies this question (`automaticallyVerified: false` for every input tested, including "hu") — the false positive was never in the scorer. It was in what happened next: the learner's own self-assessment click ("Yes, I explained it well") was recorded with `supportTier: "supported"`, correctly blocking mastery advancement (the existing, correct 007B/Decision 60 gate) — but nothing recorded that this specific attempt's correctness had never been independently verified at all, at the Evidence Confidence layer. `computeCompetencyConfidence`'s `anyEvidence` check asked only "has this question been attempted" (`timesSeen > 0`), with zero regard for who judged it correct. One self-assessed "Yes" was therefore proven sufficient, on its own, to clear the "insufficient evidence" floor and reach `confidenceTier: "low"` → `educationalState: "building-knowledge"` → the parent-facing label "Developing — Real progress. More practice will build this up further."

**Root cause, precisely stated:** `supportTier` ("independent" | "supported") had been silently carrying two different concepts — educational scaffolding (its original, correct meaning) and self-assessment provenance (a second meaning it acquired only because Tier 3/5 self-assessment happened to reuse the same field, per 007B's own contemporaneous comment: "self-assessed Tier 3/5 answers were already 'supported' before Part 5 existed"). No field anywhere recorded *who* judged an attempt correct.

---

**Correction 1 — Verification provenance (the primary fix, Parts 4-6, "Defect A"):** a new, separate, additive field — `verified` (application layer) / `last_attempt_verified` (database) — distinguishes Angel's own automatic verification from the learner's self-report, without touching `supportTier`'s existing meaning at all. Migration 076 (`supabase/migrations/076_evidence_verification_provenance.sql`, **drafted, not applied**) adds one nullable boolean column, following migration 024's exact precedent (additive, `add column if not exists`, historical rows left null/unreconstructable rather than fabricated). `lib/ali/history.ts`'s `recordOutcome()` gains an optional `verified` parameter (defaults to `true` — every existing caller's exact prior behaviour); the one call site that must pass `false` is `submitSelfAssessment()` in `app/learning-intelligence/practice/[area]/page.tsx`. `lib/ali/confidence.ts`'s `anyEvidence` check now reads `q.timesSeen > 0 && q.verified !== false` — this **narrows** what counts as clearing the floor, it never invents new evidence, and a question with `verified` omitted/undefined (every pre-migration row, Mock's own separate evidence path) is read as verified, so nothing pre-existing is retroactively downgraded.

**A second, independently duplicated evidence-reading path was found and fixed identically, not left inconsistent.** `lib/ali/persistence/competencyEvidence.ts`'s `fetchCompetencyEvidence()` — a genuinely separate query from `lib/ali/persistence/educationalStateRuntime.ts`'s `fetchCompetencyStateEvidence()`, feeding `RecommendationCandidate.confidenceTier` and `validateCompetencyMastery()` rather than the display-label path — read the exact same `times_seen`/`times_correct` columns with the same blindness to provenance. Both were corrected with the identical `verified ?? true` mapping. Both now use `select("*")` rather than a hand-picked column list (a resilience fix in its own right — an explicit list naming a column that does not yet exist would have failed the entire query outright on a database that has not applied migration 076, breaking Educational State computation for every competency, not just this one; `select("*")` degrades gracefully, matching `recordOutcome()`'s own established convention).

**A third, architecturally separate system was found to have the same class of defect and was corrected too — not left as a silent gap.** `lib/learningEngine/rollup.ts` / `lib/learningEngine/evidence.ts` (the Learning Engine V1 system that `lib/learningEngine/profile.ts` actually wires into the production Learning Report's recommendation copy, via `computeRecommendations()`) independently reads the same two raw columns. Without a fix here, the same self-assessed "hu" would have produced `outcome: "success"` and `signal: "Demonstrated"` — an even stronger unearned claim than "Developing." `deriveQuestionTypeOutcome()` gained an `anyVerified` parameter (defaults to `true`); `false` (every contributing question's most recent attempt was self-assessed, none verified) now correctly yields `"none"` rather than `"success"`/`"struggle"`. Evidence Tier (`deriveEvidenceTier`) is deliberately unaffected — Tier and Signal are independent axes by this file's own pre-existing design (Principle 3), and the raw attempt (`timesSeen`) genuinely happened, so Tier correctly still reports ET-1, "a small number of instances," while Signal now correctly reports "Not Yet Observed" rather than "Demonstrated."

**Recommendation integrity (Part 10) — proven, not separately redesigned.** `lib/ali/persistence/recommendationRuntime.ts`'s `deriveTriggerReason()` was already a correct, pure function of `EducationalState`: `"exploring"` → `"never-attempted"`, every other non-mastered state → `"weak-competency-remediation"` (the trigger behind copy like "needs more practice before it's solid"). Once Correction 1 stops a self-assessed-only attempt from reaching `"building-knowledge"`, this existing, unmodified logic already stops it from reaching `"weak-competency-remediation"` — proven with a direct unit test (`tests/lib/ali/recommendationTrigger.test.ts`), not assumed. No change was needed or made to this file's recommendation-generation logic itself (the function was exported, unchanged otherwise, purely to make this provable).

---

**Correction 2 — `checkAcceptedAnswerSet` character-substring false positives ("Defect B," Part 2), found and confirmed independently during this session's own adversarial testing, not in the original incident report.** The prior implementation matched via raw `.includes()` in both directions: `"hi"` matched inside `"...checked HIs spikes..."`; `"x"` matched inside `"excitement"`; `"up"` matched inside `"warmed UP and..."` — all purely character coincidences, none a real word. Worse, this reaches **Tier 2 and Tier 4, both fully auto-verified with no self-assessment gate at all** — a garbage two-character answer could earn full automatic marks with zero human judgement involved anywhere. Fixed in `lib/learningEngine/englishAnswerValidation.ts` with whole-word TOKEN matching (not a percentage or minimum-character heuristic): the full accepted phrase, at any length including one token, may still be found intact inside a longer user answer (rewording/expansion, always allowed); the user's own answer may be found intact inside a longer accepted phrase only when the user supplied 2+ tokens (abbreviated-but-substantial answers still credit; a single incidental word never does, since it cannot be distinguished from coincidence the way a 2+-token exact-order match can). `checkQuotationPresent` (Tier 3's verbatim-quotation check) was untouched — it was never defective, and the real CSSE mark scheme itself requires word-for-word quotation there.

**Correction 3 — the legacy English heuristic's unconditional half-marks floor, found during this session's own adversarial testing of the legacy path (Part 12), not previously known.** `lib/learningEngine/practiceContent.ts`'s `scoreEnglishAnswer()` (the pre-007B fallback, still live for the original 13 untagged English rows, reached with **zero self-assessment step of any kind** — its return value is written straight to evidence as automatically-verified marks) unconditionally returned `Math.max(1, round(maxMarks/2))` for any answer 8+ characters long that didn't reach the full-marks threshold — regardless of whether it shared any real content with the model answer at all. Proven live: `"aaaaaaaa"` scored 2 of 4 marks against a real `w1-raceday-01`-equivalent question. This is, on its own terms, a more severe defect than either Defect A or B — fully automatic, zero human or algorithmic judgement gate. Fixed with the minimal change: partial credit is now conditional on `ratio > 0` (at least one real content keyword from the model answer genuinely present); pure keyword-overlap-free input now correctly scores 0. Legitimate partial and full credit for genuinely overlapping or fully-correct differently-worded answers is unchanged and re-verified.

---

**Self-assessment UX (Part 8):** the Tier 3/5 self-assessment prompt (`app/learning-intelligence/practice/[area]/page.tsx`) is reworded so the reason is explicit — "Angel can't read and judge an explanation the way a person can, so this one needs your own honest check" — and the response is framed as an honest comparison rather than a mark the learner hands themselves ("Yes, I got the key points" replaces "Yes, I explained it well"). No internal terminology (`supportTier`, verification provenance, confidence tier, evidence tier) is exposed. The false-positive Tier 5 feedback message ("Angel recognised the feeling you named" for input `"x"`, Part 9) required no separate fix — it is generated from `namedComponentCorrect`, which Correction 2 already fixes at the source.

**Assessment Coverage copy (Part 11):** `components/learningEngine/EvidenceProfile.tsx`'s single line ("25 of 27 Question Types have content authored · 13 attempted," previously joined by a mid-dot) is split into two separately labelled facts — "Platform content available: 25 of 27 question types" / "Your child has attempted: 13 question types" — same underlying data, no model change, closing the reasonable-parent misreading identified in the prior checkpoint.

---

**Historical data (Part 7):** no historical learner evidence was rewritten, deleted, or migrated. Migration 076 is additive-only and not applied by this session. Every pre-migration/unpopulated row reads as `verified: true` by explicit design (the conservative default is documented in the migration file itself) — real historical evidence is not retroactively downgraded, and no bulk remediation of past records is proposed or needed, since this correction only narrows what counts as evidence going forward, never rewrites what was already recorded.

**Preservation of completed Stage 2 experience work (Part 14) — confirmed intact, not regressed:** Mathematics naming, the "Start Mathematics Practice" CTA, Enter-key submission and the double-submit guard (`practiceInteractionGuard.ts`, untouched by this correction), focus management, Mathematics working/explanations, English Submit-button discoverability, tablet navigation, and the canonical CSSE Submit/Next behaviour are all unchanged by this increment's diff (verified by re-running the full test suite and production build after every code change, not merely by inspection).

**Practice-system boundary (Part 13):** no consolidation was performed. Corrections 1-3 live in the canonical Practice pathway (`lib/learningEngine/englishAnswerValidation.ts`, the Educational Intelligence Engine, the Learning Engine V1 rollup) and the legacy pathway (`lib/learningEngine/practiceContent.ts`'s `scoreEnglishAnswer`, reached by `app/english/[id]/page.tsx` and others via the legacy bridge). The adaptive Mock pathway is unaffected — its own evidence path (`lib/mockAttempt/evidenceAdapter.ts`) was not touched, and Mock content remains at 0 `mock_eligible` rows, unreachable regardless.

**Validation:** TypeScript clean. Automated tests: 698/698 pass (664 pre-existing + 34 new — `tests/lib/ali/confidence.test.ts`, `tests/lib/ali/educationalState.test.ts`, `tests/lib/ali/recommendationTrigger.test.ts`, `tests/lib/learningEngine/rollup.test.ts` newly created; `englishAnswerValidation.test.ts` and `practiceContent.test.ts` extended), covering every adversarial input this increment's diagnostic and this session's own testing produced, plus the full "hu" incident reproduced end-to-end with real, unmodified production functions and its positive counterpart (a genuinely independently-verified attempt still progresses normally). Copy Quality Guard: PASS, 0 violations, 250 files. ESLint: 80 problems (62 errors, 18 warnings) — byte-identical to the pre-correction baseline; zero new issues in any file this increment touched (the 3 pre-existing errors in `app/learning-intelligence/practice/[area]/page.tsx` were confirmed, via `git show HEAD`, present on `main` before Stage 2 ever began). Production build: succeeds, every route prerenders.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim real-device/browser verification was performed (see Verdict); it does not claim migration 076 has been applied — the database still has no `last_attempt_verified` column until the Founder applies it, and every code path degrades safely in the meantime; it does not claim historical learner evidence has been reclassified or audited (a real, disclosed limitation of the last-attempt-only provenance signal, consistent with this codebase's existing `lastAttemptCorrect`/`lastAttemptSupportTier` convention, not a new inconsistency); it does not claim the three-practice-system architecture question is resolved — that remains a separate, unauthorised consolidation decision; it does not claim Stage 2 is closed; it does not begin Stage 3.

**Verdict:** the three proven false-positive defects (self-assessment provenance blindness across two parallel evidence systems; character-substring coincidence in three Tiers including two fully-automatic ones; the legacy heuristic's unconditional half-marks floor) are corrected, with positive-flexibility for genuinely valid alternative wording independently re-verified rather than assumed preserved. All code-verifiable closure gates (Part 17) PASS. **Stage 2 itself remains OPEN** — real-device/browser verification of both the original Stage 2 experience work and this correction's own self-assessment UI change has not been performed this session, and per this session's own standing closure rule, code-verifiable gates passing authorises the commit below but does not by itself close Stage 2.

**Date:** 2026-08-19

**Rationale:** The Founder's own directive was explicit that code-verifiable gates passing authorises committing this correction even while Stage 2 stays open pending device verification — recording and committing now gives the Founder a complete, reviewable artefact (real diff, real test suite, real governance trail) rather than leaving proven, fixed defects sitting uncommitted while the separate device-verification gate remains outstanding.

**Implications:** The Founder should apply migration 076 when convenient (additive-only, no data loss, every code path already tolerates its absence) to make the verification-provenance fix fully live rather than defaulting every attempt to `verified: true` until then. Real-device/browser verification of the reworded self-assessment UI, the corrected Assessment Coverage copy, and the still-outstanding original Stage 2 experience items remains the one blocker to closing Stage 2 and considering Stage 3. The legacy English path's own broader architecture (Part 12) was adversarially tested and its one found defect corrected, but a full legacy-vs-canonical consolidation decision remains separate, unauthorised, and out of this increment's scope, per Part 13.

---

### Decision 107 — Migration 076 confirmed applied to production (column exists, verified via a bounded anon-key read-only probe); historical verification-provenance forensic audit of the 458 pre-migration NULL rows completed via a Founder-run production query; migration 077 (drafted, NOT applied) reconciles the one identified, deterministically-classifiable subset — 27 English `practice_experience`/`supported` rows, split 19 auto-verified / 8 self-assessed — with row-count safety guards and full idempotence; the remaining 431 rows are architecturally confirmed to carry no self-assessment risk and are untouched

**Migration 076 production status:** the Founder applied it manually ("Success. No rows returned"). A bounded, non-destructive, anon-key-only probe (written, run, then removed — no code change resulted) confirmed `select id,last_attempt_verified,... from ali_student_question_history limit 1` returns HTTP 200 rather than a "column does not exist" error — **the column exists in production.** Row-level data (counts, types, defaults) could not be independently confirmed by this session — `ali_student_question_history` returned zero rows to every count query under the anon key (`content-range: */0` universally), a genuine, pre-existing RLS/grant restriction (sanity-checked against `ali_question_bank`, a known-public table, which correctly returned its real count of 312 rows via the identical query technique — confirming the restriction is real, not a broken probe). This matches this project's own prior documented finding (Decision 60: "`ali_student_question_history` is RLS-opaque to the anon key").

**The Founder then ran a production SQL audit directly**, resolving what this session could not reach via the anon key. Result: **458 historical rows** have `last_attempt_verified IS NULL` (expected — migration 076 is additive with no default and no backfill `UPDATE`, so every pre-existing row was left null by ordinary Postgres `ADD COLUMN` behaviour, provable from the migration's own text independent of any data access). Of these, the Founder isolated the one population genuinely ambiguous for self-assessment provenance: **27 rows** where `subject = 'english'`, `source = 'practice_experience'`, `last_attempt_support_tier = 'supported'`.

**Canonical validation-tier source, traced and confirmed:** `validationTier` is not a database column — it is a key inside `ali_question_bank.prompt`, a genuine `jsonb` column (confirmed: migration `010_ali_persistence_layer.sql:43`'s own comment; every English content migration inserts a literal `"validationTier":"..."` key into that same column; `lib/ali/questionBank.ts:17` reads it straight through with no separate mapping table). Because `prompt` is real `jsonb`, a single SQL query using `prompt->>'validationTier'` classifies every row deterministically — no two-step SQL-then-application-code process was needed, contrary to what might otherwise have been assumed necessary.

**The Founder's own follow-up production query, using exactly this extraction, classified all 27 rows with certainty:**
- **19 rows — auto-verified, never self-assessed:** `TIER2_ACCEPTED_SET` (12), `TIER4_ORDERED_LIST` (5), `TIER6_MULTI_SELECT` (2). All three tiers are unconditionally `automaticallyVerified: true` in `scoreEnglishComprehensionAnswer()` (confirmed in `tests/lib/learningEngine/englishAnswerValidation.test.ts`). `supported` on these rows means Guided Practice was toggled on for a question Angel still automatically marked — genuine guided-but-verified evidence. The two `question_type = 'multiple-choice'` rows specifically (flagged as an open question in the prior checkpoint) are now confirmed, not merely inferred, to be `TIER6_MULTI_SELECT` — migration `049_english_wave2_content.sql`'s own comment ("The 3 multi-select questions use question_type = 'multiple-choice'") and a direct read of `w2-morningpatrol-08`'s content independently corroborate this before the production query even ran.
- **8 rows — genuinely self-assessed, unverified:** `TIER3_QUOTATION_PLUS_EXPLANATION` (6), `TIER5_NAMED_COMPONENT_PLUS_EXPLANATION` (2). Both tiers are unconditionally `automaticallyVerified: false` — the only way a `supported` outcome exists for one of these rows is the learner's own self-assessment click (`submitSelfAssessment()`, which always writes `supportTier: "supported"` regardless of Guided Practice). **The original Founder-reported incident's own row is confirmed present and correctly identified within this population:** `w1-raceday-05`, `source: practice_experience`, `support_tier: supported`, `last_attempt_correct: true`, `last_attempt_verified: NULL`, `times_seen: 1`, `times_correct: 1` — exactly the shape this whole investigation predicted.

**Competency exposure, determined from the full content catalogue (not the 8 individual question IDs beyond `w1-raceday-05`, which this session was not given):** every `TIER3`/`TIER5` question in the entire authored English content bank (Wave 1 and Wave 2, cross-referenced against `ali_question_bank`'s row-level `skill` column, i.e. the real Question Type ID, across all content migrations) maps to exactly two competencies via `QUESTION_TYPE_PRIMARY_COMPETENCY` — **RC-02 (Inference and Justified Interpretation)** via `QT-RC-02`, and **RC-01 (Literal Retrieval from Narrative Text)** via `QT-RC-07`/`QT-RC-08`. No `TIER3`/`TIER5` content exists anywhere in the bank for RC-03 or RC-04 — those competencies are covered exclusively by auto-verified tiers. This is a closed, deterministic, source-derived fact, not a sample: **the self-assessment provenance risk in this entire content bank is structurally confined to RC-01 and RC-02, nowhere else.**

**Downstream persisted-state analysis (the question this decision had to answer before proposing any correction):**
1. **Yes, computed live on every read, never cached** — `lib/learningEngine/educationalIntelligenceService.ts`'s own docstring: "computed fresh from real evidence every time... never cached as an independent fact." `lib/learningEngine/preparationState.ts` confirms the same for readiness ("no new persistence, no caching beyond the request's [lifetime]"). Correcting `last_attempt_verified` is therefore sufficient on its own — no batch recomputation, cache invalidation, or reconciliation job is required; the next Learning Report read for an affected competency recomputes correctly.
2. **`ali_durable_mastery` receives a row write on every attempt** (`processEvidenceForCompetency()` calls `saveDurableMasteryRecord()` unconditionally) — but its `validated`/`durable` fields require `thresholdMet` (`validateCompetencyMastery()`, `lib/ali/masteryValidation.ts:41-45`: `thresholdMet = some question has distinctCorrectSessions >= masteryThreshold`), and `distinctCorrectSessions` only ever increments for `supportTier === "independent"` (`applyAttemptOutcome()`, `lib/ali/mastery.ts`) — a gate wholly independent of, and unaffected by, the confidence-tier defect Decision 106 corrected, and one this migration does not touch. **A self-assessed (`supported`) attempt could never, on its own, have produced `validated: true` in `ali_durable_mastery`.**
3/4. **No durable mastery transition, and therefore no `ali_educational_audit` entry, could have been produced by a self-assessed event alone** — `recordAuditIfNewlyHigherEvidence()` only fires when `educationalState` newly reaches `mastered`/`durably-mastered`, which requires the same `thresholdMet` gate. Both tables are confirmed clean of this specific defect by construction, not merely assumed — **no repair to `ali_durable_mastery` or `ali_educational_audit` is proposed or needed.**
5. No persisted readiness snapshot exists to carry a stale effect (per point 1).
6/7. Setting the 8 rows' provenance correctly will automatically correct current learner-facing state on the very next read of the affected competency (RC-02 for `w1-raceday-05`, confirmed); no separate recomputation is required.

**Source risk audit, by source, for the remaining 431 NULL rows (none touched by migration 077):** `founder_validation_assessment` (10) and `legacy_english_practice` (4) both score via the legacy heuristic (`scoreEnglishAnswer`), traced directly in `app/learning-intelligence/founder-validation/csse/page.tsx:195-199` and `app/english/[id]/page.tsx` — neither has any self-assessment UI at all. `mock_exam` (8) routes anything it cannot exact-match to admin-gated manual marking (`mock_score_attempt`, migrations 074/075), never learner self-report. Mathematics (independent and guided alike) has no self-assessment pathway anywhere in this codebase — `checkMathsAnswer()` is always exact-match automatic. English `practice_experience`/`independent` rows can never have been written by `submitSelfAssessment()`, which always writes `supported`. **Every one of these categories is architecturally confirmed clean, not assumed clean.** Family Choice pilot evidence was not traced this session and is explicitly flagged as unresolved rather than assumed safe — not part of migration 077's scope.

**Migration 077** (`supabase/migrations/077_english_supported_verification_provenance_reconciliation.sql`, **drafted, NOT applied**): two `UPDATE` statements, each scoped by an identical four-part predicate (`subject = 'english'`, `source = 'practice_experience'`, `last_attempt_support_tier = 'supported'`, `last_attempt_verified IS NULL`) plus the `prompt->>'validationTier'` tier-set discriminator — one setting `last_attempt_verified = true` for the 19-row auto-verified population, one setting it `false` for the 8-row self-assessed population. No profile IDs are hard-coded; no answer text or profile identifiers are read anywhere in the migration. **Guarded**: a `do $$ ... $$` block counts the pending rows against each predicate first — proceeds only if the count is exactly 19/8 (the Founder-verified classification); recognises 0/0 as an already-applied, safe no-op (logs, makes no changes); `RAISE EXCEPTION`s and touches nothing on any other observed count, refusing to guess if production has drifted since the forensic query ran. No other column, table, policy, or trigger is created, dropped, or altered.

**Validation:** TypeScript clean. Automated tests: 710/710 pass (12 new structural tests, `tests/supabase/englishSupportedVerificationProvenanceReconciliation.test.ts`, matching the established string/regex-against-raw-SQL convention — proving the predicate scoping, the tier-set partition, the row-count guard, the idempotent no-op branch, the refusal branch, that no answer text or profile ID is touched, and that no column other than `last_attempt_verified` is ever assigned). Copy Quality Guard: PASS, 0 violations, 250 files. ESLint: 80 problems (62/18), byte-identical to Decision 106's own baseline — zero new issues. Production build: succeeds.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim migration 077 has been applied — it is drafted for Founder review only, per explicit instruction; it does not claim the exact 8 self-assessed question IDs beyond `w1-raceday-05` were individually enumerated this session (the Founder's own query returned tier-level counts, not a per-row list — the competency-exposure conclusion above is derived from the full content catalogue, which is exhaustive for RC-01/RC-02 regardless of exactly which 8 rows they are, not from the individual unknown IDs); it does not claim the Family Choice pilot source has been audited; it does not claim historical Mathematics/Mock/legacy English evidence has been separately re-verified for unrelated defects (Correction 3's own legacy-heuristic accuracy question remains open for historical `legacy_english_practice`/`founder_validation_assessment` rows, a different concern from self-assessment provenance); it does not claim Stage 2 is closed; it does not begin Stage 3.

**Verdict:** the historical provenance question is now resolved for its one genuinely ambiguous population (27 rows), with a fully deterministic, evidence-backed classification and a migration that is narrowly scoped, guarded, and idempotent. The remaining 431 NULL rows are architecturally confirmed to carry no comparable risk. Migration 077 is ready for Founder review and application; it has not been applied. Stage 2 remains at the same status as Decision 106 — **OPEN**, pending Founder application of this migration and the still-outstanding real-device/browser verification.

**Date:** 2026-08-19

**Rationale:** Recording this now, rather than waiting for the Founder to apply migration 077, gives the Founder a complete, reviewable forensic trail (the exact classification evidence, the downstream-safety proof, the guarded migration text, and its own test suite) to review and act on at their own pace, consistent with this project's standing practice of never applying a data-mutating migration without direct Founder action.

**Implications:** The Founder should review and apply migration 077 when ready (guarded, idempotent, safe to run more than once). After application, the 8 self-assessed rows' affected competencies (RC-02 confirmed for `w1-raceday-05`; RC-01 for any of the remaining 7 that turn out to be atmosphere/character-type questions) should be spot-checked on the Learning Report to confirm they no longer show unearned progress from this specific event alone — noting explicitly that if genuine, independently-verified RC-02/RC-01 evidence exists elsewhere for the same learner, the competency's displayed state may legitimately remain unchanged, which would not indicate the correction failed. The Family Choice pilot source remains an open, untraced item for a future session if the Founder wants the same audit extended to it.

### Decision 108 — Migration 077 confirmed applied to production with an exact, Founder-verified 19/8 split (12 TIER2 + 5 TIER4 + 2 TIER6 = 19 → `verified: true`; 6 TIER3 + 2 TIER5 = 8 → `verified: false`), including the original `w1-raceday-05` incident row now correctly `last_attempt_verified: false`; **the historical educational-integrity blocker is RESOLVED**; forward-going provenance re-confirmed via existing tests (84/84 relevant, no duplication); Stage 2 is CODE COMPLETE but **not yet genuinely CLOSED** — real-device/browser verification remains the one outstanding, externally-caused gate

**Migration 077 production verification — Founder-supplied, not independently re-queried (per explicit instruction not to re-run destructive/exploratory checks against a now-settled result):**

| Tier | `last_attempt_verified` | Count |
|---|---|---|
| TIER2_ACCEPTED_SET | true | 12 |
| TIER4_ORDERED_LIST | true | 5 |
| TIER6_MULTI_SELECT | true | 2 |
| TIER3_QUOTATION_PLUS_EXPLANATION | false | 6 |
| TIER5_NAMED_COMPONENT_PLUS_EXPLANATION | false | 2 |

19 true + 8 false = 27, exactly matching migration 077's own guarded pre-application count and Decision 107's forensic classification — **no drift, no surprise, the migration behaved exactly as designed and tested.** `w1-raceday-05` (the original Founder-reported incident) now reads `source: practice_experience`, `support_tier: supported`, `last_attempt_correct: true`, **`last_attempt_verified: false`**, `times_seen: 1`, `times_correct: 1` — the row is now correctly marked as never independently verified, which is the entire point of this whole correction arc. **The historical educational-integrity blocker that has kept Stage 2 at status C since Decision 106 is now RESOLVED**, on real, Founder-confirmed production evidence, not inference.

**Forward-going provenance — re-confirmed via existing tests, none duplicated:** `tests/lib/ali/confidence.test.ts` and `tests/lib/ali/educationalState.test.ts` (both already existing from Decision 106, re-run this session, 84/84 relevant tests across the full Stage 2 educational-integrity suite pass) already prove: a self-assessed (`verified: false`) attempt cannot clear the "insufficient evidence" confidence floor on its own; a genuinely automatically-verified attempt (`verified: true`, or omitted) still does, unaffected; the full "hu" incident, replayed against real unmodified production functions, no longer reaches `building-knowledge`/"Developing"; a genuine independently-verified attempt still progresses normally. No new tests were written this session specifically to avoid duplicating this already-complete proof.

**Stage 2 implementation inventory (for verification reference, not a new scope declaration):** Mathematics naming (A), "Start Mathematics Practice" CTA (B), Enter-to-submit (C), double-submit guard (D), feedback-before-Next focus management (E), Reading textarea newline preservation (F), English Submit discoverability (G), Writing async-submission guard (H), tablet icon-rail navigation (I), Assessment Coverage two-line split (J), automatic English scoring correction — Defect B/legacy heuristic (K), verification provenance — Defect A (L), and this decision's own historical reconciliation (M) — all implemented, all re-confirmed via the full automated suite this session with zero regression.

**Real-device/browser verification — attempted once, per standing practice, and not fought further.** The Chrome extension remains unreachable in this environment (`tabs_context_mcp` returned the same "extension is not connected" result as every prior attempt this arc). No Founder-supplied real-device evidence (screenshots, described rendered behaviour, or an explicit "I checked this on my tablet" statement) was provided this session to substitute for it — production SQL evidence was supplied and used for Part 2 above, but that is a different kind of evidence than rendered-UI verification and is not treated as a substitute for it, consistent with this project's own standing practice ([[feedback_visual_gate_no_substitute]] in this session's own operating memory: hold the gate open when Chrome is down rather than substitute source review or infer a pass).

**Verdict:** Stage 2 is **CODE COMPLETE**. Every substantive concern raised across this entire arc — Practice Experience interaction quality, the English assessment-validity defect, the second (character-substring) defect, the third (legacy-heuristic) defect, verification-provenance architecture, and now historical data reconciliation — is implemented, tested, and (where production evidence was obtainable) confirmed live. **Stage 2 is not, however, being declared genuinely CLOSED this session.** The one remaining gate — real rendered verification of the responsive/interaction surfaces (tablet navigation, Mathematics CTA, English submission controls, touch targets, Learning Report rendering) — has never actually been performed by anyone, this session or any prior one in this arc, and no evidence exists yet to responsibly claim it has. This is recorded honestly as an external, mechanical, tooling-caused gap, not a substantive doubt about Stage 2's own correctness — a genuinely different category from the historical-integrity concern this decision resolves.

**Automated validation:** 710/710 tests pass (unchanged from Decision 107 — no code change this session). TypeScript clean. Copy Quality Guard: PASS, 0 violations, 250 files. ESLint: 80 problems (62/18), byte-identical to every prior checkpoint in this arc. Production build: succeeds.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim real-device/browser verification was performed; it does not claim Stage 2 is closed; it does not claim the Continuous Writing content gap (a separate, pre-existing, disclosed limitation, not a Stage 2 defect) has been resolved — it has not, and per this session's own standing instruction is not treated as a Stage 2 closure blocker either; it does not claim the Family Choice pilot source has been audited for the same provenance question; it does not begin Stage 3.

**Date:** 2026-08-19

**Rationale:** Recording the historical-integrity resolution now, on real production evidence, gives the Founder a settled, closed sub-question rather than leaving a fully-resolved matter administratively ambiguous — while continuing to withhold the word "CLOSED" from Stage 2 as a whole keeps this log's own standing discipline (visual/rendered verification is never inferred or assumed) intact, exactly as Decisions 104 and 105 each did for their own visual-verification gates.

**Founder real-device checklist — the only items genuinely still unverified, kept deliberately short:**
1. Tablet width (~768–1023px): top navigation does not truncate; "Progress" is not clipped to "Progr"; icon-only rail appears with visible accessible labels/tooltips.
2. Mathematics: "Start Mathematics Practice" is visually obvious; Enter submits; rapid double-Enter does not double-submit; feedback and working/explanation appear before Next; Tab/Enter reach and activate Next naturally.
3. English (both canonical Practice and the legacy `/english/[id]` lesson): the Submit control is reachable without scrolling to the very end of a long lesson; sticky positioning does not cover question content or get hidden behind mobile bottom navigation; the self-assessment screen (where it appears) is clear, jargon-free, and both buttons are reachable.
4. Textarea Enter creates a newline in Reading/Writing, never an accidental submit.
5. No horizontal overflow, no clipped text, comfortably tappable controls at tablet/mobile width.
6. Learning Report: "Platform content available" and "Your child has attempted" read as two distinct facts, not one line.

### Decision 109 — Founder real-production testing surfaced a "yes" answer rendering as "Correct" on a Tier 5 English question; root-caused to a presentation-layer defect (a self-assessed outcome rendered identically to a genuinely auto-verified one), NOT a scoring/evidence defect — the scoring layer already correctly refused to auto-verify "yes" throughout, confirmed by reproducing the exact question; fixed by making the rendered label honestly distinguish the two cases; Report Consistency and Recommendation Integrity investigated and found educationally valid, not defective; Stage 2 remains OPEN — substantial new Founder desktop/Mathematics/English evidence is credited, but tablet/mobile verification is still not supplied

**Founder real-production evidence (positive):** Mathematics lesson/practice transition, the Submit control, correct-answer feedback, the "You're ready to practise this properly" next-action prompt, Reading Comprehension submission/Next, the Tier 3 self-assessment UI actually rendering in production, model-answer/explanatory feedback visibility, and desktop primary navigation were all confirmed working as designed by direct Founder interaction with the real, deployed product — the first genuine rendered-evidence this arc has received for any of these surfaces. Recorded as **FOUNDER REAL-PRODUCTION EVIDENCE**, a different and higher-value category than this session's own source/automated-test verification, per the Founder's own explicit instruction this round.

**The contradiction reported:** the same apparent question, answered "yes," rendered as an unqualified "Correct" with the model answer exposed in one captured state, and correctly as "Angel didn't recognise the feeling you named... Yes, I got the key points / Not quite" in another.

**Investigation, not assumed:**
1. **Exact question identified:** `w2-pianorecital-06` — "How does Freya feel in the recital hall in the minutes before she is called, and why?" (`supabase/migrations/049_english_wave2_content.sql`), `validationTier: TIER5_NAMED_COMPONENT_PLUS_EXPLANATION`, `acceptedAnswers: ["nervous","anxious","worried","apprehensive"]`, competency RC-01 (via `QT-RC-08`).
2. **The exact literal answer "yes" was reproduced against this exact question through the real, unmodified `scoreEnglishComprehensionAnswer()`** (the only scoring function this content can reach — see point 3): `automaticallyVerified: false`, `namedComponentCorrect: false`, `earnedMarks: 0`, confirmed by a new permanent test (`tests/lib/learningEngine/englishAnswerValidation.test.ts`). **The scoring layer never auto-verified "yes" for this question, at any point relevant to this investigation.** The same battery ("no," an unrelated sentence, "hi"/"x"/"up" — the Defect B proof strings) was also reproduced: none auto-verify.
3. **Every other route that could theoretically serve this content was checked and ruled out, not assumed clean:** the legacy `/english/[id]` lesson page draws from a wholly separate, static, hand-authored dataset (`data/lessons.ts`) that does not contain this passage at all. The adaptive Mock English route (`/mocks/adaptive/english`) is firewalled to `mock_eligible` content, which remains at 0 in production — this route currently always falls back to a synthetic fixture that also does not contain this passage. **Only the canonical Practice page can have served this exact question**, and its own scoring path is the one already confirmed clean in point 2.
4. **No stale state, deployment skew, or parallel implementation was found or is required to explain the evidence.** The real mechanism, confirmed directly in source: `SubmitOrNext` (the shared feedback/Next control both auto-verified and self-assessed Reading answers render through) previously rendered the identical text "Correct" whenever `lastCorrect === true`, with no distinction for *how* that became true. A self-assessed "Yes" click (`submitSelfAssessment(true)`) legitimately, by design, sets `lastCorrect = true` — this was always the intended behaviour (Decision 106's own self-assessment policy: "preserve the learner's self-assessment"), and the underlying evidence was, throughout, correctly recorded as `last_attempt_verified: false` (confirmed by this session's own reconfirmation of the Decision 106/107/108 chain, unaffected by anything in this decision). **The defect was never in what was scored or recorded — it was that the rendered "Correct" label could not be told apart from a genuinely auto-verified one**, so a Founder (or any parent) watching the screen could reasonably read it as Angel's own independent judgement of "yes," which it never was.

**Correction:** a new pure function, `resolveOutcomeLabel()` (`lib/learningEngine/practiceInteractionGuard.ts`, alongside the existing `canSubmitAnswer`/`runGuardedSubmission` — the same "pure predicate extracted for testability without a DOM/React harness" pattern Stage 2 already established), decides the rendered outcome label from `lastCorrect` and a new, presentation-only `selfAssessed` flag. `SubmitOrNext` now renders **"You said: Correct"** (blue, distinct from the emerald genuinely-auto-verified "Correct") whenever the outcome came from the learner's own self-assessment click — plain language, no internal terminology (`supportTier`, `verified`, "confidence," "evidence tier" are never shown). A new `wasSelfAssessed` state flag (reset in `resetActivityUiState()`, set in `submitSelfAssessment()`) threads this through; Mathematics (which never self-assesses) is structurally unaffected — the prop defaults `false`. **No scoring, evidence-recording, or `last_attempt_verified` logic was touched by this correction** — this is presentation-only, exactly matching the instruction not to solve this by special-casing "yes" or the specific question ID, and not to weaken any existing validation.

**Report Consistency investigation:** traced the terminology, not assumed a bug. `deriveEvidenceSignal()` (`lib/learningEngine/rollup.ts`) answers a *direction* question ("has this competency ever succeeded"); `deriveEvidenceTier()` answers a *confidence* question ("how reliable/repeated is what we know"). A competency that has been attempted repeatedly and consistently answered wrong produces exactly "NOT YET DEMONSTRATED" (Signal: real evidence exists, but never a success) *and* "Substantiated" (Tier: that consistent-wrong pattern is itself a stable, repeated, ET-3 finding) simultaneously — this is **educationally valid and intentional, not a defect**: it means "we have strong, reliable evidence this genuinely has not been demonstrated yet," a more useful and more honest state than low-confidence uncertainty would be. The Mathematics summary line ("3 development areas · 0 of 6 not yet evidenced," `components/learningEngine/ReadinessSummary.tsx`) was checked the same way: `developmentAreas` and `notYetEvidenced` are already two genuinely distinct, non-overlapping counts (not one conflated figure, unlike the pre-Decision-106 Assessment Coverage line) — `0 not yet evidenced` here means every one of the 6 competencies already has real evidence, so none of the 3 development areas is "absence of evidence mislabeled as weakness"; each is a genuinely evidenced, repeated-struggle finding. **Classification: (A) educationally valid but not optimally communicated** — the word "Substantiated" reads to a lay parent as inherently positive, when it actually describes confidence, not valence. Recorded as a real, disclosed, non-blocking terminology-clarity item for a future bounded increment (the same category as the Continuous Writing content gap and the Family Choice pilot audit) — **not implemented this session**, per the explicit instruction not to redesign the Learning Report in this pass.

**Recommendation Integrity — reconfirmed, not re-fixed.** `computeDiagnosticFindings()` (`lib/learningEngine/diagnostics.ts:36-38`) pushes a competency into `developmentAreas` (the "Revision" category, the exact source of "needs more practice before it's solid") only when `signal === "Not Yet Demonstrated"` — and Decision 106 already made that signal correctly exclude self-assessed-only or unverified-only evidence (`deriveQuestionTypeOutcome()`'s `anyVerified` gate), proven by the existing `tests/lib/learningEngine/rollup.test.ts` end-to-end test (re-run this session, still passing, not duplicated). No self-assessed-only or absence-of-evidence competency can reach this recommendation category. No new fix was required or made here.

**Regression tests added:** 10 new tests — `resolveOutcomeLabel()`'s full decision table (`tests/lib/learningEngine/practiceInteractionGuard.test.ts`); the exact `w2-pianorecital-06` question against "yes," "no," an unrelated sentence, the full adversarial battery plus "hi"/"x"/"up", a genuine named component (positive flexibility), and a reconfirmation that Tier 2/6 auto-verification is unaffected (`tests/lib/learningEngine/englishAnswerValidation.test.ts`). React-level state reset (`resetActivityUiState()` clearing `wasSelfAssessed`/`lastCorrect` between questions, `goToNextOrFinish()` calling it synchronously before the next question renders) is verified by direct source inspection — this repository has no DOM/React rendering harness to test it any other way, consistent with `practiceInteractionGuard.ts`'s own standing documented limitation.

**Validation:** TypeScript clean. Automated tests: 720/720 pass (10 new, zero regression). Copy Quality Guard: PASS, 0 violations, 250 files. ESLint: 80 problems (62/18), byte-identical to every prior checkpoint this arc — the 3 pre-existing errors in this same file remain confirmed present on `main` before Stage 2 (re-verified `git show HEAD` this session). Production build: succeeds.

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim any scoring, evidence-recording, or verification-provenance logic was found defective or changed — none was; it does not claim the Report Consistency terminology-clarity finding has been fixed — it is recorded as a real, tracked, non-blocking item; it does not claim tablet or mobile rendered verification has been performed — the Founder's own supplied evidence this round covers desktop and the core Mathematics/English interaction flow, not tablet/mobile specifically; it does not claim Stage 2 is closed; it does not begin Stage 3.

**Verdict:** the reported contradiction is fully resolved — root-caused with certainty (not inferred), confirmed to be a presentation defect rather than a scoring/evidence one, fixed at the correct architectural layer, and regression-tested. Report Consistency and Recommendation Integrity were investigated on their own merits and found sound. **Stage 2 remains OPEN, per explicit instruction and on its own remaining merits**: real rendered evidence now exists for desktop and the core Mathematics/English flows (credited above as Founder real-production evidence, materially narrowing what Decision 108 left outstanding), but tablet and mobile — the surface that motivated Stage 2's own tablet-navigation correction in the first place — has not yet been confirmed by anyone, this session or any prior one.

**Date:** 2026-08-19

**Rationale:** Recording this now gives the Founder a complete account of a real production finding that could otherwise have been read as a serious regression — it was not, but disclosing the exact mechanism, not just the conclusion, is what this log's own standing discipline requires. Correcting the presentation gap immediately, rather than only documenting it, closes a real (if non-scoring) trust concern without waiting for a separate increment.

**Founder real-device checklist — narrowed from Decision 108's own six items, reflecting the new evidence supplied this round:**
1. **Tablet width (~768–1023px)** — still fully outstanding: top navigation does not truncate; "Progress" is not clipped; icon-only rail with accessible labels.
2. **Mobile width (~375–500px)** — still fully outstanding: no horizontal overflow, comfortably tappable controls, sticky English Submit does not collide with bottom navigation.
3. Textarea Enter creates a newline, never an accidental submit (not explicitly covered by this round's evidence).
4. The corrected self-assessment "You said: Correct" label reads clearly and is not confusing on a real device (new, from this decision).
5. Learning Report: "Platform content available" vs "Your child has attempted" read as two distinct facts.

Items 1 and 2 (tablet and mobile specifically) remain the substantive blockers — the Mathematics, English-submission, and desktop items from Decision 108's own checklist are now satisfied by this round's Founder evidence and are not repeated.

**Implications:** Once the Founder confirms tablet and mobile rendering (the two genuinely remaining items), a successor decision can record Stage 2 closure without further code investigation. The Report Consistency terminology-clarity finding and the pre-existing Continuous Writing/Family Choice items remain separately tracked for future, explicitly-authorised programme work — none is a Stage 2 blocker.

### Decision 110 — Mobile real-device gate recorded PASS on Founder Android screenshots (Mathematics, English self-assessment, Writing, Vocabulary, bottom navigation all confirmed rendering correctly, no overflow/clipping observed); Learn-path (`app/english/[id]`) educational-integrity investigation found "Lesson Complete" copy already correctly evidence-neutral about correctness, but uncovered and fixed a third, previously-unfixed copy of Decision 106's Correction 3 defect in the Learn-path's own local scorer — latent, not exploitable by the exact reported inputs, but real; Stage 2 remains OPEN pending tablet verification only

**Mobile real-device gate: PASS**, supported by Founder-supplied real Android screenshots — recorded as **FOUNDER REAL-DEVICE EVIDENCE**, the same category Decision 109 established for the desktop/Mathematics/English round. Confirmed rendering correctly: Mathematics question cards within viewport, usable answer fields and feedback, incorrect-answer feedback, Show/Hide working, step-by-step working with no visible horizontal overflow, Next Question reachable above the fixed navigation, Creative Writing introduction/prompt/writing area, a readable and touch-accessible writing checklist, Vocabulary content and flashcard CTA, and stable bottom navigation. **Not claimed from this evidence: tablet verification** — mobile and tablet remain distinct gates, per explicit instruction, and only mobile is closed by this round's screenshots.

**Learn-path investigation (`app/english/[id]/page.tsx`), traced not assumed:**

A. **Route/component/data source:** `app/english/[id]/page.tsx`, content from `data/lessons.ts` — a wholly separate, static, hand-authored dataset from `ali_question_bank`, confirmed in Decision 109's own investigation and reconfirmed here. A locally-defined `scoreAnswer()` (lines ~59-77, its own diverged copy of the keyword-overlap heuristic, not imported from `lib/learningEngine/practiceContent.ts`) scores every question in the lesson.

B. **Answer treatment:** automatically scored via keyword-overlap heuristic — never self-assessed, never review-only for the *evidence write itself*. "Lesson Complete! You answered X of Y questions" (the copy the Founder specifically flagged) is computed purely from `answeredCount` — whether a field was non-empty — with **no reference to correctness at all**, confirmed directly in source (`app/english/[id]/page.tsx`'s `handleSubmit`/completion render). This copy was correct as originally written and was not changed, per the explicit instruction not to alter accurate wording.

C. **Downstream evidence behaviour, traced through `recordLegacyPracticeEvidence()` (`lib/learningEngine/legacyPracticeEvidence.ts`):** every question in the lesson — answered or not — triggers a `recordOutcome()`/`processEvidenceForCompetency()` call attempt on submit, **but only if `params.questionId` resolves to a real, tagged row in `ali_question_bank`** (`resolveBankEvidenceContext()`, this module's own pure FK-safety boundary, now covered by new tests — it had none before). Most of `data/lessons.ts`'s question IDs are untagged; for those, the function returns `{ recorded: false, outcome: "untagged-question" }` before touching `ali_student_question_history`, `ali_durable_mastery`, `ali_educational_audit`, confidence, Educational State, or any recommendation/readiness surface — **evidence-neutral by construction for untagged content**, not by assumption. **Migration 013 does tag exactly 5 legacy English rows** (`eng-001-q2`, `eng-001-q3`, `eng-002-q1`, `eng-002-q3`, `eng-003-q3`, marks 2/3/4/2/3 respectively) — for these specific 5, and only these, real evidence writes do occur, scored by the local `scoreAnswer()`.

D. **"Lesson Complete" is activity-completion-only**, confirmed by C above: nothing downstream reads `answeredCount` or the completion screen's own render as an educational-success signal. Evidence is written per-question, independently, gated entirely by tag-status and the scorer's own `isCorrect` result — never by whether the lesson as a whole was "completed."

E. **Reproduced directly against the real, unmodified (at investigation start) `scoreAnswer()`:** "Gree" and "Yuoye" (the exact Founder-reported inputs) both score 0 against every marks value 1-4 — too short (4/5 characters) to reach the keyword-overlap branch at all (an 8-character floor gates entry). A genuine strong answer still earns full marks (reconfirmed by test). **A defect was found, not by the exact reported inputs, but by the same class of adversarial testing this whole arc has repeatedly applied**: `scoreAnswer()`'s final fallback unconditionally returned `Math.max(1, round(maxMarks/2))` for ANY 8+ character answer with zero real keyword overlap — the exact same defect as Decision 106's Correction 3, in a third, independently-diverged, never-before-fixed copy. For a hypothetical 1-mark tagged legacy English question, `Math.max(1, round(1/2)) = 1 = maxMarks`, so gibberish could flip `isCorrect: earned === q.marks` to `true` — a genuine false-positive evidence write. **Not currently exploitable**: none of the 5 rows actually tagged today carries exactly 1 mark, confirmed by direct inspection of migration 013. This is disclosed as a real, latent defect that would become exploitable the instant any 1-mark legacy English row is tagged, with zero code change required to trigger it — not smoothed over as "not applicable" merely because today's specific content set happens not to trigger it.

**Correction:** the identical minimal fix as Correction 3 — partial credit is now conditional on `ratio > 0` (genuine keyword overlap), never awarded unconditionally by length alone — applied to this local copy, preserving its own pre-existing extra guards unchanged. No consolidation with the canonical `scoreEnglishAnswer()` or `scoreEnglishComprehensionAnswer()` was performed — Learn, Practice, and Mock remain three distinct implementations, per explicit instruction not to force Learn to behave like Practice or to redesign Learn.

**Regression tests added:** 10 — `tests/lib/learningEngine/legacyEnglishLessonScoring.test.ts` (the fix itself: zero-overlap garbage now scores 0 at every marks value 1-4, the exact "Gree"/"Yuoye" inputs reconfirmed harmless regardless of marks, the specific 1-mark exploit path closed by name, genuine partial/full credit preserved) and `tests/lib/learningEngine/legacyPracticeEvidence.test.ts` (new — this module had no prior coverage: `resolveBankEvidenceContext()`'s untagged/unmapped/genuinely-tagged cases, proving the evidence-neutrality boundary directly rather than by narrative).

**Validation:** TypeScript clean. Automated tests: 730/730 pass (10 new). Copy Quality Guard: PASS, 0 violations, 250 files. ESLint: 80 problems (62/18), byte-identical to every prior checkpoint this arc. Production build: succeeds, `/english/[id]` still builds and prerenders correctly with `scoreAnswer` now exported for testability (the same pattern already established for `resolveOutcomeLabel`/`canSubmitAnswer`).

**What this decision does NOT claim:** it does not claim the reported "Gree"/"Yuoye" inputs themselves ever produced false evidence — they did not, confirmed directly; it does not claim Learn was redesigned or made to behave like Practice — it was not; it does not claim the untagged majority of `data/lessons.ts` content has been reviewed for tagging correctness — that is a separate, unauthorised content question; it does not claim tablet verification has been performed; it does not claim Stage 2 is closed; it does not begin Stage 3.

**Verdict:** the Learn path is now confirmed evidence-neutral for its untagged majority (proven, not assumed) and free of the one latent scoring defect found in its tagged minority. The mobile real-device gate is genuinely satisfied. **Stage 2 remains OPEN — tablet verification (~768–1023px: top navigation, "Progress" not clipped, icon-only rail) is now the one remaining substantive item**, everything else Decision 108/109 tracked having been either resolved by correction or satisfied by Founder-supplied evidence this round and the last.

**Date:** 2026-08-19

**Rationale:** Recording the mobile PASS and the Learn-path finding together keeps this session's full account in one place — a genuine gate closing and a genuine, if latent, defect found and fixed in the same investigation, neither smoothed into the other.

**Implications:** Founder tablet-width evidence (or working browser tooling) is now the single item standing between Stage 2 and closure. If that is supplied and passes, a successor decision can close Stage 2 without further code investigation — every other gate this arc has raised is now either fixed, tested, or confirmed by real evidence.

### Decision 111 — Founder real-device tablet evidence supplied (19 August 2026): tablet gate PASS; **STAGE 2 IS APPROVED AND CLOSED** by explicit Founder decision; two non-blocking cosmetic observations recorded for future work, not acted on; Stage 3 authorised, not begun

**Founder real-device tablet evidence (19 August 2026)**, recorded as **FOUNDER REAL-DEVICE TABLET EVIDENCE** — the last item Decision 110 left outstanding. Screenshots of the live Mathematics practice experience on a real tablet, landscape orientation, confirm: tablet navigation rendering, Mathematics practice question cards, question progress, timer, answer entry, the Check Answer action, on-screen keyboard interaction, and continued visibility of the active question and answer field while the keyboard is open. **No blocking clipping, overflow, or interaction failure is evidenced. Tablet gate: PASS.**

Two observations — desktop-style tablet navigation, and subdued secondary/disabled text in dark appearance — are recorded as **non-blocking future UX refinement only**, per explicit Founder instruction. Not investigated, not actioned, not treated as a closure blocker.

**Stage 2 closure checklist, each item resolved across this arc and not re-litigated here:**
- Educational-integrity verification — Decisions 106, 109, 110 (self-assessment provenance, the presentation-label defect, the Learn-path legacy-scorer defect).
- Historical/provenance investigation and reconciliation — Decisions 106-108 (migrations 076/077, the 19/8 forensic classification, `w1-raceday-05` confirmed corrected in production).
- Previously identified scoring defects — all three (character-substring coincidence, the canonical legacy-heuristic floor, the Learn-path's own diverged copy of the same floor) corrected and regression-tested.
- Evidence boundaries — verified: `ali_durable_mastery`/`ali_educational_audit` proven incapable of being populated by self-assessed-only evidence (Decision 107); the Learn path's untagged majority proven evidence-neutral by construction (Decision 110).
- Automated regression verification — 730/730 tests passing as of `cd71e27`, reconfirmed clean this session (no code changed).
- Production build verification — succeeded at every checkpoint this arc, most recently in Decision 110.
- Desktop, mobile, and now tablet Founder real-device verification — complete (Decisions 109, 110, this decision).

**No code, test, or configuration change accompanies this decision** — closure is a documentation-only act on an already-fully-verified baseline, per explicit instruction not to modify production behaviour or perform cosmetic work as part of closure. Minimum verification re-run to confirm the baseline is undisturbed: TypeScript clean, 730/730 tests pass (identical to Decision 110's own count) — full lint/Copy-Guard/build were not re-run, since no source file changed and Decision 110 already confirmed all four clean against this exact commit.

**Verdict: STAGE 2 IS APPROVED AND CLOSED**, by explicit Founder decision, on the evidence recorded across Decisions 104-110 and this decision's own tablet confirmation. Every gate this arc raised — interaction quality, three independent scoring defects, verification-provenance architecture, historical data reconciliation, and desktop/mobile/tablet rendered verification — is resolved, fixed, tested, or confirmed by real evidence, not assumed.

**Date:** 2026-08-19

**Rationale:** Recording closure now, against the complete real-device evidence set this arc has assembled, gives the Founder a single, definitive close-out artefact for a programme that spanned repeated real production findings, three genuine scoring defects, and a full historical reconciliation — each disclosed and resolved in turn rather than smoothed over.

**Implications:** **Stage 3 is authorised** by this decision. It has not begun — no Stage 3 scope, design, or implementation work was performed as part of this closure, per explicit instruction. The two non-blocking cosmetic observations (tablet navigation styling, dark-mode secondary-text contrast) and the previously-tracked non-blocking items (Continuous Writing content gap, Family Choice pilot provenance audit, the Report Consistency terminology-clarity finding) remain open, separately-tracked backlog items for whatever future, explicitly-authorised work the Founder chooses — none reopens or qualifies this closure.
